  // Space / Enter only â€” Escape is owned by registerCancel.
  window.addEventListener("keydown", onAuthoringKeys);

  /* â”€â”€ Gesture dispatch â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */

  function handleAuthoringGesture(g: Gesture): boolean {
    // Grip drag owns its own gesture from drag-start to drag-end.
    if (g.kind === "drag-start") {
      const grip = gripAt(g.event);
      if (grip && (activeTool === "select" || activeTool === null)) {
        const pt = groundPoint(g.event);
        if (pt) {
          startGripDrag(grip, pt);
          ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "grabbing";
          return true;
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-move" && gripDrag) {
      const pt = groundPoint(g.event);
      if (pt) updateGripDrag(pt);
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-end" && gripDrag) {
      endGripDrag();
      ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "";
      return true;
    }

    // Plain left click â€” place, select, or modify.
    if (g.kind === "click" && g.button === 0) {
      return handleLeftClick(g.event, g.mods);
    }

    return false;
  }

  function handleLeftClick(e: PointerEvent, mods: Mods): boolean {
    const forceSelect = mods.ctrl || mods.meta || mods.shift || mods.alt;
    const selectMode = !activeTool || activeTool === "select" || forceSelect;

    if (selectMode) {
      if (pickSelect(e)) return true;
      if (!activeTool || activeTool === "select") return true;
      // Fall through to tool placement when force-select found no element.
    }

    if (!activeTool) return true;

    if (
      activeTool === "move" || activeTool === "copy" ||
      activeTool === "rotate" || activeTool === "mirror" ||
      activeTool === "array" || activeTool === "offset"
    ) {
      const pt = groundPoint(e);
      if (pt) onModifyClick(pt, e);
      return true;
    }

    if (activeTool === "door" || activeTool === "window") {
      onDrawClick(new THREE.Vector3(), e);
      return true;
    }

    const pt = groundPoint(e);
    if (pt) onDrawClick(pt, e);
    return true;
  }

  /* â”€â”€ Hover (preview + snap indicator) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */

  function handleAuthoringHover(e: PointerEvent): void {
    if (gripDrag) return;

    // Snap indicator updates regardless of tool.
    try {
      if (ctx.world?.camera?.three) {
        const ray = new THREE.Raycaster();
        ray.setFromCamera(ndcOf(e), ctx.world.camera.three);
        const hits = ctx.groundPlane ? ray.intersectObjects([ctx.groundPlane], false) : [];
        const base = hits.length ? hits[0].point.clone() : null;
        void updateSnapAsync(e, base);
      }
    } catch { /* */ }

    if (!activeTool) return;
    if (
      activeTool === "select" || activeTool === "move" || activeTool === "copy" ||
      activeTool === "rotate" || activeTool === "mirror" ||
      activeTool === "array" || activeTool === "offset"
    ) return;

    updatePreview(e);
  }

  /* â”€â”€ Cancel (Escape) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */

  function handleAuthoringCancel(): boolean {
    try {
      if ((window as any).__obsPlanVisible?.()) return false;
    } catch { /* */ }

    const hasSomething =
      (activeTool !== null && activeTool !== "select") ||
      chainStart !== null ||
      poly.length > 0 ||
      pendingOp.kind !== "none" ||
      gripDrag !== null;

    if (!hasSomething) {
      // Already select with nothing in-progress: clear selection like before.
      if (selectedIds.size) {
        selectedIds.clear();
        applySelection();
        refreshGrips();
        paletteTarget = null;
        notifyAuthoringChange();
        updateLabel();
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

    chainStart = null;
    chainStartDir = null;
    poly = [];
    pendingOp = { kind: "none" };
    clearPreviews();
    try { accuDraw.end(); } catch { /* */ }
    try { snapping.hideIndicator(); } catch { /* */ }

    if (gripDrag) {
      try { endGripDrag(); } catch { /* */ }
      ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "";
    }

    const preserved = lastCommand;
    setAuthoringTool("select");
    lastCommand = preserved;
    notify("info", "Cancelled â€” Select");
    try { (window as any).obsRefreshRibbon?.(); } catch { /* */ }
    return true;
  }

  /* â”€â”€ Key handling (Space / Enter only â€” Escape is in the router) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */

  function onAuthoringKeys(e: KeyboardEvent): void {
    const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName?.toLowerCase();
    if (tag === "input" || tag === "textarea" || (e.target as HTMLElement)?.isContentEditable) return;

    try { if ((window as any).__obsPlanVisible?.()) return; } catch { /* */ }

    if (e.key === "Backspace" || e.code === "Space" || e.key === " ") {
      if (tag === "button") return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      const cmd = lastCommand;
      if (!cmd) {
        notify("info", "No previous command to repeat â€” pick a tool first");
        return;
      }
      try {
        setAuthoringTool(cmd);
        notify("success", `Repeat: ${cmd}`);
        try { (window as any).obsRefreshRibbon?.(); } catch { /* */ }
        try {
          import("../core/context").then(({ ctx }) => {
            if ((ctx.features as any)?.planView?.isVisible?.()) {
              (ctx.features as any).planView.setTool?.(cmd);
            }
          }).catch(() => {});
        } catch { /* */ }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[authoring] repeat failed", err);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (e.key === "Enter" && activeTool) {
      if (
        activeTool === "wall" || activeTool === "beam" || activeTool === "strip" ||
        activeTool === "mline" || activeTool === "duct" || activeTool === "pipe" ||
        activeTool === "cable-tray" || activeTool === "conduit" ||
        activeTool === "brace" || activeTool === "truss" || activeTool === "grid"
      ) {
        chainStart = null;
        chainStartDir = null;
        clearPreviews();
        try { accuDraw.end(); } catch { /* */ }
        updateLabel();
      }
      if (activeTool === "slab") finishSlab();
    }
  }

  // Snaps ON with all modes + visible markers
  try {
    snapping.enableAllTypes();
    snapping.setEnabled(true);
    snapping.setGridSize(0.1);
    snapping.setPixelTolerance(18);
  } catch { /* */ }

  // Default tool = Select so drawn elements are immediately clickable
  try {
    setAuthoringTool("select");
  } catch { /* */ }

  const api: AuthoringService & Record<string, any> = {
    isActive: isDrawingActive,
    getActiveTool, setAuthoringTool, setLevel, getLevel,
    getActivePreset, getLastCommand, setLastCommand,
    getElementCount, getElements, getSelectedIds,
    getElementById,
    pickAuthoredElementAt,
    deleteSelectedElement, clearAllElements, clearDatums,
    rebuildAuthored, transformElements,
    selectAllAuthored,
    setSelectedAuthored,
    getElementWorldBox,
    getAuthoredGroup,

    pinSelected,
    createSimilarFromSelection,
    splitSelectedAtMidpoint,
    joinSelectedWalls,
    alignSelectedAxis,

    getWallLocationLine,
    setWallLocationLine: (v) => setWallLocationLine(v as any),
    getWallChain, setWallChain,
    getWallOffset, setWallOffset,
    getWallJoinStatus,
    setWallJoinStatus: (v) => setWallJoinStatus(v as any),

    getGrids, setGrids, getLevels, setLevels,
    getActiveLevelId, setActiveLevelById,
    getActiveFamilyId, getActiveFamilyTypeId, setActiveFamily,
    syncFamilyInstances,

    onAuthoringChange,
    migrateMepFittingRadii,
  };
  ctx.features.authoring = api;
  register("authoring", api);
  try { (window as any).__obsAuthoring = api; } catch { /* */ }
}

===== FILE: src/features/revit-experience.ts =====
/**
 * Open BIM Studio v48 â€” Revit Experience Layer
 *
 * This module is deliberately UI/interaction focused. Existing authoring,
 * selection, snapping, properties and plan engines remain the source of truth;
 * this layer makes them behave like one coherent BIM authoring environment:
 *
 *   Ribbon â†’ contextual ribbon â†’ options bar â†’ canvas â†’ view control bar â†’ status bar
 *
 * It adds:
 *   - command registry and command order semantics
 *   - selection-aware contextual commands
 *   - Revit-style options bar / type selector
 *   - modify contextual ribbon
 *   - temporary review states (hide/isolate/reveal)
 *   - view control bar
 *   - keyboard semantics for Escape / Space / Enter / Delete / Tab / Shift
 *   - selection preselection tooltip and focus feedback
 *   - a full tool/catalog index used by the command palette
 *
 * It intentionally does not claim to reproduce Autodesk's proprietary RVT
 * database/family solver. It reproduces the user-facing command grammar using
 * the openBIM engines already present in the application.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";

  getActiveTool,

  setAuthoringTool,

  getElementById,

  rebuildAuthored,

} from "./authoring";
import { history } from "./history";

export type ExperienceMode =
  | "idle"
  | "place"
  | "modify"
  | "annotate"
  | "query"
  | "review";

export interface RevitCommand {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  ribbon?: string;
  group?: string;
  keywords?: string[];
  shortcut?: string;
  when?: () => boolean;
  run: () => void | Promise<void>;
}

interface SelectionSummary {
  fragments: number;
  authored: number;
  authoredTypes: string[];
  firstName: string | null;
}

interface ExperienceState {
  mode: ExperienceMode;
  commandId: string | null;
  commandLabel: string | null;
  previousTab: string;
  activeTab: string;
  optionsVisible: boolean;
  contextualVisible: boolean;
  temporaryMode: "none" | "isolate" | "hide" | "reveal";
  scale: string;
  detail: "Coarse" | "Medium" | "Fine";
  discipline: "Coordination" | "Architecture" | "Structure" | "Mechanical" | "Electrical" | "Plumbing";
  crop: boolean;
}

const state: ExperienceState = {
  mode: "idle",
  commandId: null,
  commandLabel: null,
  previousTab: "architecture",
  activeTab: "modify",
  optionsVisible: true,
  contextualVisible: false,
  temporaryMode: "none",
  scale: "1:100",
  detail: "Medium",
  discipline: "Coordination",
  crop: true,
};

const commands = new Map<string, RevitCommand>();
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

let optionsBar: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let contextualBar: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let viewBar: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let promptBar: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let hoverCard: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let installed = false;
let hoveredAuthorId: string | null = null;
let lastSelectionCount = 0;

function emit() {
  for (const cb of listeners) {
    try { cb(); } catch { /* UI listener must never crash authoring */ }
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("obs:revit-experience", { detail: { ...state } }));
}

export function onRevitExperienceChange(cb: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(cb);
  return () => listeners.delete(cb);
}

export function registerRevitCommand(command: RevitCommand): void {
  commands.set(command.id, command);
}
export function registerRevitCommands(list: RevitCommand[]): void {
  for (const command of list) registerRevitCommand(command);
}
export function listRevitCommands(): RevitCommand[] { return [...commands.values()]; }

function toast(message: string, kind: "info" | "success" | "warn" = "info", ms = 1800) {
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("obs-toast", { detail: { message, kind, ms } }));
}

function el<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(tag: K, cls?: string): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) node.className = cls;
  return node;
}

function button(label: string, icon: IconName | string, onclick: () => void | Promise<void>, active = false): HTMLButtonElement {
  const b = el("button", "obs-rv-btn");
  b.type = "button";
  b.dataset.active = active ? "1" : "0";
  b.title = label;
  if (icon.length <= 3) {
    b.textContent = icon;
  } else {
    try {
      b.innerHTML = `${svgIcon(icon as IconName)}<span>${label}</span>`;
    } catch {
      b.textContent = `${icon} ${label}`;
    }
  }
  if (icon.length <= 3) {
    b.innerHTML = `<span class="obs-rv-btn__glyph">${icon}</span><span>${label}</span>`;
  }
  b.onclick = async (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    try { await onclick(); } catch (err) { console.error("[revit-experience] command", label, err); toast(`${label} failed`, "warn", 2500); }
    refresh();
  };
  return b;
}

function separator(): HTMLElement {
  return el("span", "obs-rv-sep");
}

function smallLabel(text: string): HTMLElement {
  const x = el("span", "obs-rv-label");
  x.textContent = text;
  return x;
}

function selectControl<T extends string>(value: T, items: T[], onChange: (value: T) => void, width = 120): HTMLSelectElement {
  const s = el("select", "obs-rv-select");
  s.style.width = `${width}px`;
  for (const item of items) {
    const o = el("option");
    o.value = item;
    o.textContent = item;
    s.appendChild(o);
  }
  s.value = value;
  s.onchange = () => onChange(s.value as T);
  return s;
}

function numberControl(value: number, suffix: string, onChange: (v: number) => void, step = 0.01): HTMLElement {
  const wrap = el("label", "obs-rv-number");
  const input = el("input");
  input.type = "number";
  input.step = String(step);
  input.value = String(value);
  input.title = suffix;
  input.onchange = () => onChange(Number(input.value));
  const s = el("span");
  s.textContent = suffix;
  wrap.append(input, s);
  return wrap;
}

function selectionSummary(): SelectionSummary {
  const ids = getAuthoredSelection();
  const byType = new Set<string>();
  let name: string | null = null;
  for (const id of ids) {
    const e = getElementById(id);
    if (!e) continue;
    byType.add(e.type);
    if (!name) name = e.name;
  }
  return {
    fragments: selection.size(),
    authored: ids.length,
    authoredTypes: [...byType],
    firstName: name,
  };
}

function getModeForTool(tool: string | null): ExperienceMode {
  if (!tool) return selectionSummary().fragments || selectionSummary().authored ? "modify" : "idle";
  if (["move", "copy", "rotate", "mirror", "array", "offset"].includes(tool)) return "modify";
  if (["select"].includes(tool)) return "modify";
  if (["mline"].includes(tool)) return "annotate";
  if (["dim-aligned", "dim-linear", "dim-angular", "spot-elevation", "tag", "room-tag", "material-tag", "text"].includes(tool)) return "annotate";
  return "place";
}

function updateModeFromState() {
  state.mode = getModeForTool(getActiveTool());
  // Modify contextual bar is opt-in only (ribbon "Modify bar").
  // Do not auto-open it on select / right-click.
  if (getActiveTool()) {
    state.commandId = `tool.${getActiveTool()}`;
    state.commandLabel = labelForTool(getActiveTool()!);
  } else {
    state.commandId = null;
    state.commandLabel = null;
  }
}

function labelForTool(tool: string): string {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    wall: "Wall", slab: "Floor", column: "Column", beam: "Beam", brace: "Brace", truss: "Truss",
    footing: "Isolated Foundation", strip: "Wall Foundation", door: "Door", window: "Window",
    duct: "Duct", pipe: "Pipe", "cable-tray": "Cable Tray", conduit: "Conduit", equipment: "Component",
    grid: "Grid", level: "Level", move: "Move", copy: "Copy", rotate: "Rotate", mirror: "Mirror",
    array: "Array", offset: "Offset", mline: "Detail Line", select: "Modify",
  };
  return map[tool] ?? tool;
}

function activateTool(tool: string, toastText?: string) {
  setAuthoringTool(tool as any);
  state.commandId = `tool.${tool}`;
  state.commandLabel = labelForTool(tool);
  state.mode = getModeForTool(tool);
  if (toastText) toast(toastText, "success");
  emit();
}

function repeatLast() {
  const cmd = getLastCommand();
  if (!cmd) {
    toast("No previous command to repeat.");
    return;
  }
  activateTool(cmd, `Repeat: ${labelForTool(cmd)}`);
}

function clearSelection() {
  selection.clear();
  void selection.applyToHighlighter();
  setAuthoringTool("select");
  refresh();
}

function isolateSelection() {
  const authored = getAuthoredSelection();
  const snap = selection.snapshot();
  if (!authored.length && selection.isEmpty()) { toast("Select elements first", "warn"); return; }
  try {
    (ctx.hider as any)?.isolate?.(snap);
  } catch {
    try { (ctx.hider as any)?.isolate?.(snap, true); } catch { /* SDK drift */ }
  }
  state.temporaryMode = "isolate";
  toast("Temporary Hide/Isolate â†’ Isolate", "success");
  refresh();
}

function hideSelection() {
  const snap = selection.snapshot();
  if (!selection.isEmpty()) {
    try { (ctx.hider as any)?.set?.(snap, false); } catch {
      try { (ctx.hider as any)?.hide?.(snap); } catch { /* SDK drift */ }
    }
  }
  state.temporaryMode = "hide";
  toast("Selected elements hidden temporarily", "success");
  refresh();
}

function revealAll() {
  try { (ctx.hider as any)?.show?.(); } catch { /* SDK drift */ }
  state.temporaryMode = "reveal";
  toast("Reveal Hidden Elements", "success");
  refresh();
}

function focusSelection() {
  if (selection.isEmpty() && getAuthoredSelection().length === 0) {
    fitAll();
    return;
  }
  void selection.focus();
}

function execute(id: string): void {
  const command = commands.get(id);
  if (!command) return;
  if (command.when && !command.when()) return;
  void command.run();
}


let dialogHost: HTMLDivElement | null = null;

function ensureDialogHost(): HTMLDivElement {
  if (dialogHost) return dialogHost;
  dialogHost = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog-host");
  dialogHost.dataset.visible = "0";
  document.body.appendChild(dialogHost);
  return dialogHost;
}

function closeDialog() {
  if (!dialogHost) return;
  dialogHost.dataset.visible = "0";
  dialogHost.innerHTML = "";
}

function openDialog(title: string, subtitle: string, body: HTMLElement, footer?: HTMLElement): void {
  const host = ensureDialogHost();
  host.innerHTML = "";
  const card = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog");
  const header = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__header");
  const titleWrap = el("div");
  const eyebrow = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__eyebrow");
  eyebrow.textContent = "OPEN BIM STUDIO Â· REVIT EXPERIENCE";
  const h = el("h2");
  h.textContent = title;
  const p = el("p");
  p.textContent = subtitle;
  titleWrap.append(eyebrow, h, p);
  const close = el("button", "obs-revit-dialog__close");
  close.textContent = "Ã—";
  close.onclick = closeDialog;
  header.append(titleWrap, close);
  const bodyWrap = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__body");
  bodyWrap.appendChild(body);
  card.appendChild(header);
  card.appendChild(bodyWrap);
  if (footer) card.appendChild(footer);
  host.appendChild(card);
  host.dataset.visible = "1";
}

function openTypeProperties(): void {
  const ids = getAuthoredSelection();
  const type = ids.length ? getElementById(ids[0])?.type : getActiveTool();
  if (!type) {
    toast("Select an authored element or start a placement tool", "warn");
    return;
  }
  const defaults = getTypeDefaults(type);
  const body = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog-grid");
  const fields: Array<{ label: string; key: string; value: number; step: number }> = [
    { label: "Height", key: "height", value: Number(defaults.params.height ?? 3), step: 0.01 },
    { label: "Thickness / Depth", key: "thickness", value: Number(defaults.params.thickness ?? .2), step: 0.01 },
    { label: "Width", key: "width", value: Number(defaults.params.width ?? .9), step: 0.01 },
    { label: "Elevation", key: "elevation", value: Number(defaults.params.elevation ?? 0), step: 0.01 },
  ];
  for (const f of fields) {
    const row = el("label", "obs-revit-field");
    const text = el("span");
    text.textContent = f.label;
    const input = el("input");
    input.type = "number";
    input.step = String(f.step);
    input.value = String(f.value);
    input.dataset.key = f.key;
    row.append(text, input);
    body.appendChild(row);
  }
  const colorRow = el("label", "obs-revit-field");
  const colorText = el("span"); colorText.textContent = "Graphics color";
  const color = el("input"); color.type = "color"; color.value = defaults.color ?? "#cccccc";
  colorRow.append(colorText, color); body.appendChild(colorRow);

  const footer = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__footer");
  const hint = el("span", "obs-revit-dialog__hint"); hint.textContent = `${type} type defaults â€¢ affects future placement`;
  const cancel = button("Cancel", "Ã—", closeDialog);
  const apply = button("Apply", "âœ“", () => {
    for (const input of Array.from(body.querySelectorAll("input[type=number]"))) {
      const i = input as HTMLInputElement;
      const key = i.dataset.key;
      if (key) setTypeDefault(type, "params", key, Number(i.value));
    }
    setTypeDefault(type, "root", "color", color.value);
    toast(`Type Properties applied: ${type}`, "success");
    closeDialog();
  }, true);
  footer.append(hint, cancel, apply);
  openDialog("Type Properties", `Edit the family/type-style defaults for ${type}.`, body, footer);
}

  cats.appendChild(openTree);

  footer.append(hint, close);
  openDialog("Visibility / Graphics", "Control view-specific visual appearance and presentation behavior.", body, footer);
}

interface ViewTemplateSnapshot {
  name: string;
  createdAt: number;
  visual: ReturnType<typeof getVisualPreset>;
  background: ReturnType<typeof getSceneBackground>;
  transparency: number;
  sun: boolean;
  detail: ExperienceState["detail"];

const VT_KEY = "obs.revit.view-templates.v1";
function loadViewTemplates(): ViewTemplateSnapshot[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(VT_KEY);
    const arr = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}
function saveViewTemplates(list: ViewTemplateSnapshot[]) {
  try { localStorage.setItem(VT_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch { /* */ }
}
function openViewTemplates(): void {
  const body = el("div", "obs-revit-template-list");
  const list = loadViewTemplates();
  const render = () => {
    body.innerHTML = "";
    if (!list.length) {
      const empty = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__empty");
      empty.textContent = "No view templates saved. Create one from the current view settings.";
      body.appendChild(empty);
    }
    for (const t of list) {
      const row = el("div", "obs-revit-template-row");
      const text = el("div");
      const n = el("b"); n.textContent = t.name;
      const d = el("span"); d.textContent = `${t.visual} Â· ${t.background} Â· ${t.detail} Â· ${t.discipline}`;
      text.append(n, d);
      const apply = button("Apply", "âœ“", () => {
        setVisualPreset(t.visual); setSceneBackground(t.background); setSceneTransparency(t.transparency); setSunEnabled(t.sun);
        state.detail = t.detail; state.discipline = t.discipline; refresh(); toast(`Applied view template: ${t.name}`, "success");
      });
      const del = button("Delete", "Ã—", () => { const i = list.findIndex(x => x.name === t.name); if (i >= 0) list.splice(i, 1); saveViewTemplates(list); render(); });
      row.append(text, apply, del); body.appendChild(row);
    }
  };
  render();
  const footer = el("div", "obs-revit-dialog__footer");
  const name = el("input", "obs-revit-template-name") as HTMLInputElement;
  name.placeholder = "New view template name";
  const create = button("Create from Current View", "ï¼‹", () => {
    const n = name.value.trim(); if (!n) { toast("Enter a template name", "warn"); return; }
    list.unshift({ name: n, createdAt: Date.now(), visual: getVisualPreset(), background: getSceneBackground(), transparency: getSceneTransparency(), sun: isSunEnabled(), detail: state.detail, discipline: state.discipline });
    saveViewTemplates(list); name.value = ""; render(); toast(`Created view template: ${n}`, "success");
  }, true);
  const close = button("Close", "Ã—", closeDialog);
  footer.append(name, create, close);
  openDialog("View Templates", "Apply consistent view appearance and coordination standards across views.", body, footer);
}

function intersectXZ(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3, c: THREE.Vector3, d: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 | null {
  const x1 = a.x, z1 = a.z, x2 = b.x, z2 = b.z, x3 = c.x, z3 = c.z, x4 = d.x, z4 = d.z;
  const den = (x1-x2)*(z3-z4) - (z1-z2)*(x3-x4);
  if (Math.abs(den) < 1e-9) return null;
  const px = ((x1*z2-z1*x2)*(x3-x4) - (x1-x2)*(x3*z4-z3*x4)) / den;
  const pz = ((x1*z2-z1*x2)*(z3-z4) - (z1-z2)*(x3*z4-z3*x4)) / den;
  return new THREE.Vector3(px, a.y, pz);
}
function trimExtendSelected(): number {
  const ids = getAuthoredSelection();
  if (ids.length !== 2) { toast("Trim/Extend requires exactly two authored line elements", "warn"); return 0; }
  const a = getElementById(ids[0]);
  const b = getElementById(ids[1]);
  if (!a?.params.start || !a?.params.end || !b?.params.start || !b?.params.end) { toast("Both elements must be line-based", "warn"); return 0; }
  const ap1 = new THREE.Vector3(a.params.start[0], 0, a.params.start[1]);
  const ap2 = new THREE.Vector3(a.params.end[0], 0, a.params.end[1]);
  const bp1 = new THREE.Vector3(b.params.start[0], 0, b.params.start[1]);
  const bp2 = new THREE.Vector3(b.params.end[0], 0, b.params.end[1]);
  const hit = intersectXZ(ap1, ap2, bp1, bp2);
  if (!hit) { toast("Elements are parallel â€” no trim/extend intersection", "warn"); return 0; }
  const nearEnd = (p1: THREE.Vector3, p2: THREE.Vector3) => p1.distanceTo(hit) < p2.distanceTo(hit) ? "start" : "end";
  const ae = nearEnd(ap1, ap2); const be = nearEnd(bp1, bp2);
  const before = JSON.stringify([a.params, b.params]);
  if (ae === "start") a.params.start = [hit.x, hit.z]; else a.params.end = [hit.x, hit.z];
  if (be === "start") b.params.start = [hit.x, hit.z]; else b.params.end = [hit.x, hit.z];
  const after = JSON.stringify([a.params, b.params]);
  history.push({
    label: "Trim / Extend",
    apply: () => { a.params = JSON.parse(after)[0]; b.params = JSON.parse(after)[1]; rebuildAuthored(); markAuthoringDirty(); },
    revert: () => { const old = JSON.parse(before); a.params = old[0]; b.params = old[1]; rebuildAuthored(); markAuthoringDirty(); },
  });
  rebuildAuthored(); markAuthoringDirty();
  toast("Trim / Extend applied", "success");
  return 1;
}

function createCommandCatalog() {
  const regTool = (tool: string, label: string, ribbon: string, group = "Create", shortcut?: string) =>
    registerRevitCommand({ id: `tool.${tool}`, label, ribbon, group, shortcut, run: () => activateTool(tool), keywords: [label, ribbon, "create", "place"] });

  const architecture: [string, string, string][] = [
    ["wall", "Wall", "Architecture"], ["slab", "Floor", "Architecture"], ["roof", "Roof", "Architecture"],
    ["ceiling", "Ceiling", "Architecture"], ["door", "Door", "Architecture"], ["window", "Window", "Architecture"],
    ["equipment", "Component", "Architecture"], ["grid", "Grid", "Architecture"], ["level", "Level", "Architecture"],
  ];
  for (const [tool, label, ribbon] of architecture) createOrAlias(tool, label, ribbon, regTool);

  const structure: [string, string][] = [
    ["column", "Structural Column"], ["beam", "Beam"], ["brace", "Brace"], ["truss", "Truss"],
    ["footing", "Isolated Foundation"], ["strip", "Wall Foundation"],
  ];
  for (const [tool, label] of structure) regTool(tool, label, "Structure", "Framing");

  const mep: [string, string, string][] = [
    ["duct", "Duct", "Mechanical"], ["pipe", "Pipe", "Plumbing"], ["cable-tray", "Cable Tray", "Electrical"],
    ["conduit", "Conduit", "Electrical"], ["equipment", "Mechanical Equipment", "Mechanical"],
  ];
  for (const [tool, label, ribbon] of mep) createOrAlias(tool, label, ribbon, regTool);

  const modify: [string, string, string][] = [
    ["move", "Move", "Modify"], ["copy", "Copy", "Modify"], ["rotate", "Rotate", "Modify"],
    ["mirror", "Mirror", "Modify"], ["array", "Array", "Modify"], ["offset", "Offset", "Modify"],
  ];
  for (const [tool, label, ribbon] of modify) registerRevitCommand({
    id: `modify.${tool}`, label, ribbon, group: "Modify", keywords: ["modify", tool], run: () => activateTool(tool),
    when: () => getAuthoredSelection().length > 0 || !selection.isEmpty(),
  });

  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.alignX", label: "Align â€” Horizontal", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", keywords: ["align", "horizontal"], run: () => { const n = alignSelectedAxis("x"); toast(n ? "Aligned selected elements" : "Select two or more authored elements", n ? "success" : "warn"); } });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.alignZ", label: "Align â€” Vertical", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", keywords: ["align", "vertical"], run: () => { const n = alignSelectedAxis("z"); toast(n ? "Aligned selected elements" : "Select two or more authored elements", n ? "success" : "warn"); } });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.split", label: "Split Element â€” Midpoint", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", keywords: ["split", "break"], run: () => toast(splitSelectedAtMidpoint() ? "Element split" : "Select a line-based authored element", "info") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.join", label: "Join / Heal Walls", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", keywords: ["join", "wall", "heal"], run: () => toast(joinSelectedWalls() ? "Wall joins processed" : "Select joinable walls", "info") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.pin", label: "Pin", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Constraints", keywords: ["pin", "lock"], run: () => toast(pinSelected(true) ? "Pinned" : "Nothing to pin", "success") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.unpin", label: "Unpin", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Constraints", keywords: ["unpin", "unlock"], run: () => toast(pinSelected(false) ? "Unpinned" : "Nothing to unpin", "success") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.createSimilar", label: "Create Similar", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Create", keywords: ["similar", "duplicate", "place same type"], run: () => toast(createSimilarFromSelection() ? "Create Similar â€” click to place the same type" : "Select an authored element first", "info") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.delete", label: "Delete", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", shortcut: "Del", keywords: ["delete", "remove"], run: () => deleteSelectedElement() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.trimExtend", label: "Trim / Extend to Corner", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Modify", keywords: ["trim", "extend", "corner"], run: () => { trimExtendSelected(); }, when: () => getAuthoredSelection().length === 2 });

  // Annotation family â€” every entry maps to the plan tool where supported.
  const annotate: [string, string][] = [
    ["dim-aligned", "Aligned Dimension"], ["dim-linear", "Linear Dimension"], ["dim-angular", "Angular Dimension"],
    ["spot-elevation", "Spot Elevation"], ["tag", "Tag by Category"], ["room-tag", "Room Tag"],
    ["material-tag", "Material Tag"], ["text", "Text Note"], ["mline", "Detail Line"],
  ];
  for (const [tool, label] of annotate) registerRevitCommand({
    id: `annotate.${tool}`, label, ribbon: "Annotate", group: "Annotation", keywords: ["annotation", label],
    run: () => { const pv = (ctx.features as any).planView; if (pv?.isVisible?.()) pv.setTool?.(tool); else activateTool(tool); },
  });

  // View / review.
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.properties", label: "Properties", ribbon: "View", group: "Windows", shortcut: "PP", run: () => openPropertyPalette() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.projectBrowser", label: "Project Browser", ribbon: "View", group: "Windows", run: () => toggleProjectBrowser() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.modelTree", label: "Model Categories / Browser", ribbon: "View", group: "Windows", run: () => toggleModelTreePanel() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.plan", label: "Floor Plan", ribbon: "View", group: "Create Views", run: () => togglePlanView() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.schedule", label: "Schedule / Quantities", ribbon: "View", group: "Create Views", run: () => toggleSchedules() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.sheet", label: "Sheet", ribbon: "View", group: "Create Views", run: () => toggleSheetsPanel() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.issues", label: "Issues / BCF", ribbon: "Collaborate", group: "Coordination", run: () => toggleIssuesPanel() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.ids", label: "IDS Validation", ribbon: "Manage", group: "QA", run: () => toggleIdsPanel() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.timeline", label: "Phasing / 4D", ribbon: "View", group: "Coordination", run: () => toggleTimelinePanel() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.graphics", label: "Visibility / Graphics", ribbon: "View", group: "Graphics", run: openVisibilityGraphics });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.templates", label: "View Templates", ribbon: "View", group: "Graphics", run: openViewTemplates });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "modify.typeProperties", label: "Type Properties", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Properties", run: openTypeProperties });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.fit", label: "Zoom to Fit", ribbon: "View", group: "Navigation", shortcut: "ZF", run: () => fitAll() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.home", label: "Default 3D View", ribbon: "View", group: "Navigation", shortcut: "3D", run: () => goHome() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.top", label: "Top / Plan Orientation", ribbon: "View", group: "Orientation", run: () => gotoDir(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)) });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.front", label: "Front Elevation", ribbon: "View", group: "Orientation", run: () => gotoDir(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1)) });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.ortho", label: "Orthographic", ribbon: "View", group: "Projection", run: () => { void setProjection("orthographic"); } });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "view.perspective", label: "Perspective", ribbon: "View", group: "Projection", run: () => { void setProjection("perspective"); } });

  // Temporary review / graphics.
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "review.isolate", label: "Temporary Isolate", ribbon: "View", group: "Temporary", run: isolateSelection });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "review.hide", label: "Temporary Hide", ribbon: "View", group: "Temporary", run: hideSelection });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "review.reveal", label: "Reveal Hidden Elements", ribbon: "View", group: "Temporary", run: revealAll });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "review.focus", label: "Zoom to Selection", ribbon: "View", group: "Navigation", run: focusSelection });

  // Global orders.
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.select", label: "Modify / Select", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Selection", shortcut: "Esc", run: () => setAuthoringTool("select") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.repeat", label: "Repeat Last Command", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Selection", shortcut: "Space", run: repeatLast });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.cancel", label: "Cancel Current Command", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Selection", shortcut: "Esc", run: () => setAuthoringTool("select") });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.selectAll", label: "Select All", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Selection", shortcut: "Ctrl+A", run: () => void selection.selectAll() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.clearSelection", label: "Clear Selection", ribbon: "Modify", group: "Selection", run: clearSelection });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.undo", label: "Undo", ribbon: "File", group: "Edit", shortcut: "Ctrl+Z", run: () => void history.undo() });
  registerRevitCommand({ id: "global.redo", label: "Redo", ribbon: "File", group: "Edit", shortcut: "Ctrl+Y", run: () => void history.redo() });
}

function createOrAlias(tool: string, label: string, ribbon: string, regTool: (tool: string, label: string, ribbon: string, group?: string, shortcut?: string) => void) {
  if (tool === "roof" || tool === "ceiling") {
    registerRevitCommand({ id: `tool.${tool}`, label, ribbon, group: "Build", run: () => activateTool(tool) });
    return;
  }
  regTool(tool, label, ribbon, "Build");
}

function contextActions(): RevitCommand[] {
  const s = selectionSummary();
  if (!s.authored && !s.fragments) return [];
  const out: RevitCommand[] = [];
  const add = (id: string) => {
    const c = commands.get(id);
    if (c) out.push(c);
  };
  add("modify.move");
  add("modify.copy");
  add("modify.rotate");
  add("modify.mirror");
  add("modify.array");
  add("modify.alignX");
  add("modify.alignZ");
  add("modify.pin");
  add("modify.unpin");
  add("modify.createSimilar");
  add("modify.delete");
  return out;
}

function renderContextual() {
  if (!contextualBar) return;
  contextualBar.innerHTML = "";
  contextualBar.dataset.visible = state.contextualVisible ? "1" : "0";
  if (!state.contextualVisible) return;

  const summary = selectionSummary();
  const title = el("div", "obs-rv-context-title");
  const label = summary.authoredTypes.length === 1 ? labelForTool(summary.authoredTypes[0]) : "Multiple Elements";
  title.innerHTML = `<span class="obs-rv-context-title__accent">Modify |</span> ${label}`;
  contextualBar.appendChild(title);
  contextualBar.appendChild(separator());

  for (const c of contextActions()) {
    const b = button(c.label, "â€¢", () => c.run(), false);
    contextualBar.appendChild(b);
  }
  contextualBar.appendChild(separator());
  contextualBar.appendChild(button("Properties", "P", () => openPropertyPalette(), isPropertyPaletteVisible()));
  contextualBar.appendChild(button("Type Properties", "T", openTypeProperties, false));
  contextualBar.appendChild(button("Visibility / Graphics", "VG", openVisibilityGraphics, false));
  contextualBar.appendChild(button("Browser", "B", () => toggleProjectBrowser(), isProjectBrowserVisible()));
  contextualBar.appendChild(button("Zoom Selection", "Z", focusSelection, false));
}

function renderOptionsBar() {
  if (!optionsBar) return;
  optionsBar.innerHTML = "";
  optionsBar.dataset.visible = state.optionsVisible ? "1" : "0";

  const mode = state.mode;
  if (mode === "idle" && !state.contextualVisible) {
    optionsBar.append(smallLabel("Options Bar"), separator(), smallLabel("Select a tool or element to see contextual options"));
    return;
  }

  const tool = getActiveTool();
  const commandLabel = tool ? labelForTool(tool) : "Modify";
  const header = el("div", "obs-rv-options-title");
  header.textContent = commandLabel;
  optionsBar.append(header, separator());

  if (mode === "place") {
    if (!tool) return;
    optionsBar.append(smallLabel("Type"));
    const type = selectControl(commandLabel, [commandLabel, "Basic Type", "Type Library"], (v) => {
      if (v !== commandLabel) toast(`${v} selector opened`);
    }, 140);
    optionsBar.append(type, separator());

    optionsBar.append(numberControl(tool === "wall" ? getWallOffset() : 0, "Offset", (v) => {
      if (tool === "wall") setWallOffset(v); else setTypeDefault(tool, "params", "offset", v);
    }));
    optionsBar.append(numberControl(Number(getTypeDefaults(tool).params.height ?? 3), "Height", (v) => setTypeDefault(tool, "params", "height", v)));
    optionsBar.append(numberControl(Number(getTypeDefaults(tool).params.elevation ?? 0), "Elevation", (v) => setTypeDefault(tool, "params", "elevation", v)));
    optionsBar.append(separator());
    optionsBar.append(button("Chain", "â†—", () => { setWallChain(!getWallChain()); toast(`Chain ${getWallChain() ? "ON" : "OFF"}`, "info", 1200); }, getWallChain()));
    optionsBar.append(button("Multiple", "âˆž", () => toast("Multiple placement toggled", "info", 1200), false));
    optionsBar.append(button("Flip / Space", "â†»", () => toast("Flip / orientation toggled", "info", 1200), false));
    return;
  }

  if (mode === "annotate") {
    optionsBar.append(smallLabel("Reference"), selectControl("Element", ["Element", "Face", "Work Plane", "None"], () => {}, 105));
    optionsBar.append(numberControl(0.45, "Offset", () => {}));
    optionsBar.append(button("Leader", "â†—", () => toast("Leader toggled", "info", 1200), false));
    optionsBar.append(button("Multiple", "âˆž", () => toast("Multiple annotation mode", "info", 1200), false));
    return;
  }

  // Modify options.
  optionsBar.append(smallLabel("Selection"), `${selectionSummary().authored + selectionSummary().fragments} element(s)` as any, separator());
  optionsBar.append(button("Constrain", "âŠ™", () => toast("Constraint mode â€” click target geometry", "info"), false));
  optionsBar.append(button("Disjoin", "âŠ˜", () => toast("Disjoin mode toggled", "info"), false));
  optionsBar.append(button("Pin", "â—", () => { pinSelected(true); refresh(); }, false));
  optionsBar.append(button("Unpin", "â—‹", () => { pinSelected(false); refresh(); }, false));
  optionsBar.append(button("Create Similar", "ï¼‹", () => { const t = createSimilarFromSelection(); if (t) toast(`Create Similar â€” ${labelForTool(t)}`, "success"); }, false));
  optionsBar.append(separator());
  optionsBar.append(button("Properties", "P", () => openPropertyPalette(), isPropertyPaletteVisible()));
  optionsBar.append(button("Type Properties", "T", openTypeProperties, false));
}

function renderViewBar() {
  if (!viewBar) return;
  viewBar.innerHTML = "";

  const spacer = el("span");
  spacer.style.flex = "1";
  viewBar.append(spacer);

  const label = (t: string) => smallLabel(t);
  viewBar.append(label("Scale"), selectControl(state.scale, ["Fit", "1:20", "1:50", "1:100", "1:200", "1:500"], (v) => { state.scale = v; emit(); }, 90), separator());
  viewBar.append(label("Detail"), selectControl(state.detail, ["Coarse", "Medium", "Fine"], (v) => { state.detail = v as any; emit(); }, 80), separator());
  viewBar.append(label("Discipline"), selectControl(state.discipline, ["Coordination", "Architecture", "Structure", "Mechanical", "Electrical", "Plumbing"], (v) => { state.discipline = v as any; emit(); }, 112), separator());
  viewBar.append(button("Shaded", "S", () => setVisualPreset("shaded"), getVisualPreset() === "shaded"));
  viewBar.append(button("Shaded + Edges", "SE", () => setVisualPreset("shaded-edges"), getVisualPreset() === "shaded-edges"));
  viewBar.append(button("Wireframe", "W", () => setVisualPreset("wireframe"), getVisualPreset() === "wireframe"));
  viewBar.append(button("Hidden Line", "HL", () => setVisualPreset("hidden-line"), getVisualPreset() === "hidden-line"));
  viewBar.append(separator());
  viewBar.append(button("Crop", "âŒ—", () => { state.crop = !state.crop; emit(); }, state.crop));
  viewBar.append(button("Temporary Hide/Isolate", "â—Œ", () => { state.temporaryMode = state.temporaryMode === "none" ? "isolate" : "none"; emit(); }, state.temporaryMode !== "none"));
  viewBar.append(button("Reveal", "R", revealAll, state.temporaryMode === "reveal"));
  viewBar.append(button("Sun", "â˜€", () => setSunEnabled(!isSunEnabled()), isSunEnabled()));
  viewBar.append(button("VG", "VG", openVisibilityGraphics, false));
  viewBar.append(button("Templates", "VT", openViewTemplates, false));
  viewBar.append(separator());
  viewBar.append(button("3D", "3D", () => { void setProjection("perspective"); goHome(); }, getProjection() === "perspective"));
  viewBar.append(button("Ortho", "ORTHO", () => { void setProjection("orthographic"); }, getProjection() === "orthographic"));
  viewBar.append(button("Top", "TOP", () => gotoDir(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)), false));
  viewBar.append(button("Fit", "F", fitAll, false));
}

function ensureBars() {
  if (!ctx.rightHost) return;
  if (optionsBar) return;

  optionsBar = el("div", "obs-revit-options");
  contextualBar = el("div", "obs-revit-contextual");
  viewBar = el("div", "obs-revit-viewbar");
  promptBar = el("div", "obs-revit-prompt");
  hoverCard = el("div", "obs-revit-hovercard");

  try {
    state.optionsVisible = localStorage.getItem("obs.optionsBar.visible") !== "0";
  } catch { /* */ }
  optionsBar.dataset.visible = state.optionsVisible ? "1" : "0";
  contextualBar.dataset.visible = "0";
  promptBar.dataset.visible = "0";
  hoverCard.dataset.visible = "0";
  viewBar.dataset.visible = "1";
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem("obs.viewBar.visible") === "0") viewBar.dataset.visible = "0";
  } catch { /* */ }

  ctx.rightHost.append(contextualBar, optionsBar, viewBar, promptBar, hoverCard);

  // Panels own their scroll â€” do not bubble wheel to 3D zoom
  for (const bar of [optionsBar, contextualBar, viewBar]) {
    bar.addEventListener("wheel", (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); }, { passive: true });
  }
}

function updatePrompt() {
  if (!promptBar) return;
  const tool = getActiveTool();
  if (!tool) {
    promptBar.dataset.visible = "0";
    return;
  }
  const hints: Record<string, string> = {
    wall: "Place Wall: click start point â†’ click endpoint. Click again to chain. Enter finishes the chain.",
    slab: "Place Floor: click boundary points. Click the first point or press Enter to finish.",
    door: "Place Door: hover a host wall â†’ click to place. Move along the wall to adjust offset.",
    window: "Place Window: hover a host wall â†’ click to place. Move along the wall to adjust offset.",
    duct: "Place Duct: click start â†’ endpoint. Continue clicking to route; bends create fittings.",
    pipe: "Place Pipe: click start â†’ endpoint. Continue clicking to route; bends create fittings.",
    move: "Move: select element(s) â†’ base point â†’ target point. Hold Shift for orthogonal constraint.",
    copy: "Copy: select element(s) â†’ base point â†’ target point. Space repeats the order.",
    rotate: "Rotate: select element(s) â†’ center â†’ reference â†’ target angle.",
    mirror: "Mirror: select element(s) â†’ two points defining the mirror axis.",
    array: "Array: select element(s) â†’ base point â†’ set count / spacing.",
    offset: "Offset: pick a host â†’ pick the side / distance. Esc cancels the active step.",
    select: "Modify: click to select. Ctrl adds, Shift toggles, Alt removes. Tab cycles candidates.",
  };
  promptBar.textContent = hints[tool] ?? `${labelForTool(tool)} â€” click in the model to continue.`;
  promptBar.dataset.visible = "1";
}

function refresh() {
  updateModeFromState();
  ensureBars();
  renderContextual();
  renderOptionsBar();
  renderViewBar();
  updatePrompt();
  emit();
}

async function preselectionTooltip(e: PointerEvent) {
  if (!hoverCard || !ctx.rightHost) return;
  if (getActiveTool() && getActiveTool() !== "select") {
    hoverCard.dataset.visible = "0";
    return;
  }
  const canvas = (ctx.world?.renderer?.three as any)?.domElement;
  if (!canvas || e.target !== canvas) {
    hoverCard.dataset.visible = "0";
    return;
  }

  // Authored geometry is cheap to test with screen-space boxes.
  const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
  const pointer = new THREE.Vector2(
    ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1,
    -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1,
  );
  const cam = ctx.world?.camera?.three;
  if (!cam) return;
  const ray = new THREE.Raycaster();
  ray.setFromCamera(pointer, cam);
  let best: { id: string; dist: number; name: string; type: string } | null = null;
  for (const obj of getElements()) {
    const node = ctx.world.scene.three.getObjectByName(obj.id);
    if (!node) continue;
    const hits = ray.intersectObject(node, true);
    if (!hits.length) continue;
    const d = hits[0].distance;
    if (!best || d < best.dist) best = { id: obj.id, dist: d, name: obj.name, type: obj.type };
  }
  if (!best) {
    hoverCard.dataset.visible = "0";
    hoveredAuthorId = null;
    return;
  }
  hoveredAuthorId = best.id;
  hoverCard.dataset.visible = "1";
  hoverCard.style.left = `${Math.max(12, e.clientX - rect.left + 14)}px`;
  hoverCard.style.top = `${Math.max(12, e.clientY - rect.top + 14)}px`;
  hoverCard.innerHTML = `<b>${escapeHtml(best.name)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(best.type)}</span><em>Tab: cycle Â· Click: select</em>`;
}

function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" } as Record<string, string>)[c]);
}

function keydown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
  const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
  const tag = target?.tagName?.toLowerCase();
  if (tag === "input" || tag === "textarea" || target?.isContentEditable) return;

  // Centralize common Revit-style global orders, while letting active authoring
  // keep ownership of its detailed mouse/Enter/Escape state machine.
  if (e.ctrlKey && !e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === "z") return;
  if (e.ctrlKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === "y") return;

  if (e.key === "Tab" && !e.shiftKey) {
    if (!isPlanViewVisible() && getActiveTool() === "select" && hoveredAuthorId) {
      const authored = getElements();
      const idx = authored.findIndex((x) => x.id === hoveredAuthorId);
      if (idx >= 0 && authored.length > 1) {
        const next = authored[(idx + 1) % authored.length];
        if (next) {
          // Use authored selection when possible; fragment selection is handled by the native highlighter.
          window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("obs:select-authored", { detail: { id: next.id } }));
          toast(`Preselection cycle: ${next.name}`, "info", 900);
          e.preventDefault();
        }
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  if (e.key === "Escape") {
    if (state.temporaryMode !== "none") {
      revealAll();
      state.temporaryMode = "none";
      refresh();
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    return;
  }

  if (e.key === "Delete") {
    if (getAuthoredSelection().length || !selection.isEmpty()) {
      deleteSelectedElement();
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
  }

  if (e.key === "Shift") {
    const tool = getActiveTool();
    if (tool && tool !== "select") snapping.setOrtho(true, null);
  }
}

function keyup(e: KeyboardEvent) {
  if (e.key === "Shift") snapping.clearOrtho();
}

function selectionChanged() {
  const n = selectionSummary();
  const count = n.fragments + n.authored;
  if (count !== lastSelectionCount) {
    lastSelectionCount = count;
    refresh();
  } else {
    renderContextual();
    renderOptionsBar();
  }
}

function authoringChanged() {
  refresh();
}

export function setupRevitExperience() {
  if (installed) return;
  installed = true;
  createCommandCatalog();
  for (const c of commands.values()) {
    registerPaletteCommand({
      id: `revit.${c.id}`,
      label: c.label,
      category: c.ribbon ? `Revit ${c.ribbon}` : "Revit",
      hint: c.group,
      kbd: c.shortcut,
      run: () => c.run(),
      when: c.when,
    });
  }
  ensureBars();
  selection.onChange(selectionChanged);
  // Re-render the Options Bar and contextual ribbon whenever the active
  // authoring tool changes (ribbon click, Space-repeat, Esc-to-Select),
  // not only when the selection changes.
  onAuthoringChange(refresh);
  window.addEventListener("keydown", keydown, true);
  window.addEventListener("keyup", keyup, true);
  if (ctx.rightHost) {
    ctx.rightHost.addEventListener("pointermove", preselectionTooltip, true);
    ctx.rightHost.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => { if (hoverCard) hoverCard.dataset.visible = "0"; });
  }
  window.addEventListener("obs:select-authored", (e: Event) => {
    const id = (e as CustomEvent).detail?.id as string | undefined;
    if (!id) return;
    setSelectedAuthored([id], "replace");
    setAuthoringTool("select");
    openPropertyPalette();
    refresh();
  });
  window.addEventListener("obs:revit-command", (e: Event) => {
    const id = (e as CustomEvent).detail?.id as string | undefined;
    if (id) execute(id);
  });

  (ctx.features as any).revitExperience = {
    execute,
    refresh,
    activateTool,
    getState: () => ({ ...state }),
    toggleContextualBar,
    toggleOptionsBar,
    toggleViewBar,
    isContextualBarVisible,
    isOptionsBarVisible,
    isViewBarVisible,
  };
  (window as any).__obsRevitExperience = {
    execute,
    refresh,
    getState: () => ({ ...state }),
    commands,
    activateTool,
  };
  refresh();
  console.log("[Revit Experience] v48 interaction layer online â€” command grammar + contextual UX + options bar + view control bar");
}


/** Show / hide the Options Bar (Revit Options Bar under the ribbon). */
export function toggleOptionsBar(force?: boolean): boolean {
  state.optionsVisible = force != null ? force : !state.optionsVisible;
  if (optionsBar) optionsBar.dataset.visible = state.optionsVisible ? "1" : "0";
  try { localStorage.setItem("obs.optionsBar.visible", state.optionsVisible ? "1" : "0"); } catch { /* */ }
  emit();
  return state.optionsVisible;
}
export function isOptionsBarVisible(): boolean { return state.optionsVisible; }

/** Show / hide the Modify contextual bar (selection tools under ribbon). */
export function toggleContextualBar(force?: boolean): boolean {
  // When forced open, allow even without selection; auto mode still uses selection
  state.contextualVisible = force != null ? force : !state.contextualVisible;
  if (force === true) {
    // pin open
    (state as any)._contextualPinned = true;
  } else if (force === false) {
    (state as any)._contextualPinned = false;
  } else if ((state as any)._contextualPinned) {
    (state as any)._contextualPinned = false;
  }
  if (contextualBar) {
    contextualBar.dataset.visible = state.contextualVisible ? "1" : "0";
    if (state.contextualVisible) renderContextual();
  }
  try { localStorage.setItem("obs.contextualBar.visible", state.contextualVisible ? "1" : "0"); } catch { /* */ }
  emit();
  return state.contextualVisible;
}
export function isContextualBarVisible(): boolean {
  return !!(contextualBar && contextualBar.dataset.visible === "1");
}

/** View control bar (bottom of viewport scale/detail/discipline). */
export function toggleViewBar(force?: boolean): boolean {
  if (!viewBar) return false;
  const on = force != null ? force : viewBar.dataset.visible !== "1";
  viewBar.dataset.visible = on ? "1" : "0";
  try { localStorage.setItem("obs.viewBar.visible", on ? "1" : "0"); } catch { /* */ }
  return on;
}
export function isViewBarVisible(): boolean {
  return !!(viewBar && viewBar.dataset.visible !== "0");
}

export function getRevitExperienceState(): ExperienceState { return { ...state }; }
export function executeRevitCommand(id: string): void { execute(id); }


===== FILE: src/features/revit-input-parity.ts =====
/**
 * Revit Input Parity - keyboard shortcuts + mouse/view help that match
 * typical Revit navigation and command keys.
 *
 * Mouse map is owned by navigation.ts (already Revit-correct).
 * This module:
 *   1. Registers missing Revit-style two-letter + view shortcuts
 *   2. Re-asserts CameraControls mouse buttons stay OFF (nav owns them)
 *   3. Exposes a help overlay (Ctrl+/ or from Command Center)
 */

import { ctx } from "../core/context";

type Any = Record<string, any>;

let shortcutsRegistered = false;
let helpEl: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
let helpStyle = false;

/* -- Helpers --------------------------------------------------------------- */

function isTypingTarget(t: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  const el = t as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!el) return false;
  const tag = (el.tagName || "").toLowerCase();
  if (tag === "input" || tag === "textarea" || tag === "select") return true;
  if ((el as Any).isContentEditable) return true;
  if (typeof el.closest === "function" &&
      el.closest('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable="true"]')) return true;
  return false;
}

async function cam(): Promise<Any> {
  try {
    return await import("./camera-tools");
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function notify(kind: string, message: string): void {
  try {
    window.dispatchEvent(
      new CustomEvent("obs-toast", { detail: { kind, message, timeout: 1600 } }),
    );
  } catch { /* */ }
}

function getAuthoring(): Any | null {
  return (ctx.features as Any)?.authoring ?? null;
}

function selIds(): string[] {
  try {
    const sel = (ctx.features as Any)?.selection;
    return sel?.getSelectedIds?.() ?? [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/* -- Shortcut registration ------------------------------------------------- */

async function registerRevitShortcuts(): Promise<void> {
  if (shortcutsRegistered) return;
  let register: ((s: Any) => void) | null = null;
  try {
    const mod: Any = await import("./shortcuts");
    register = mod.shortcuts?.register?.bind(mod.shortcuts) ?? null;
  } catch {
    return;
  }
  if (!register) return;
  shortcutsRegistered = true;

  const reg = (s: Any) => {
    try {
      register!(s);
    } catch { /* */ }
  };

  // -- View / camera (Revit: ZA ZF ZE ZO ZI) ------------------------------
  reg({
    id: "revit-za",
    label: "Zoom All (Revit ZA)",
    keys: "za",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.fitAll?.();
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-zf",
    label: "Zoom to Fit (Revit ZF)",
    keys: "zf",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.fitAll?.();
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-ze",
    label: "Zoom Extents (Revit ZE)",
    keys: "ze",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.fitAll?.();
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-zo",
    label: "Zoom Out (Revit ZO)",
    keys: "zo",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.zoomBy?.(1.25);
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-zi",
    label: "Zoom In (Revit ZI)",
    keys: "zi",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.zoomBy?.(0.8);
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-vn",
    label: "Default 3D View (Revit VN)",
    keys: "vn",
    category: "view",
    handler: async () => {
      const m = await cam();
      m.goHome?.();
    },
  });

  // -- Temporary hide / isolate (Revit HH HI HR) --------------------------
  reg({
    id: "revit-hh",
    label: "Hide Element (Revit HH)",
    keys: "hh",
    category: "view",
    handler: () => {
      const ids = selIds();
      (window as Any).__obsVisibility?.hideElements?.(ids);
      notify("info", ids.length ? `Hidden ${ids.length}` : "Select elements first");
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-hi",
    label: "Isolate Element (Revit HI)",
    keys: "hi",
    category: "view",
    handler: () => {
      const ids = selIds();
      (window as Any).__obsVisibility?.isolateElements?.(ids);
      notify("info", ids.length ? `Isolated ${ids.length}` : "Select elements first");
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-hr",
    label: "Reset Temporary Hide/Isolate (Revit HR)",
    keys: "hr",
    category: "view",
    handler: () => {
      (window as Any).__obsVisibility?.resetTemporary?.();
      notify("success", "Visibility reset");
    },
  });

  // -- Modify (Revit MV RO AL MA CS GP) -----------------------------------
  reg({
    id: "revit-mv",
    label: "Move (Revit MV)",
    keys: "mv",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      try {
        const gizmo = (ctx.features as Any)?.gizmo;
        gizmo?.setMode?.("translate");
        notify("info", "Move mode");
      } catch { /* */ }
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-ro",
    label: "Rotate (Revit RO)",
    keys: "ro",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      try {
        const gizmo = (ctx.features as Any)?.gizmo;
        gizmo?.setMode?.("rotate");
        notify("info", "Rotate mode");
      } catch { /* */ }
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-al",
    label: "Align (Revit AL)",
    keys: "al",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      const n = (window as Any).__obsAlign?.alignSelection?.("x", "min") ?? 0;
      notify(n ? "success" : "info", n ? `Aligned ${n}` : "Select elements to align");
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-ma",
    label: "Match Properties (Revit MA)",
    keys: "ma",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      (window as Any).__obsRevitParity?.matchPropertiesPickSource?.();
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-cs",
    label: "Create Similar (Revit CS)",
    keys: "cs",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      (window as Any).__obsRevitParity?.createSimilar?.();
    },
  });
  reg({
    id: "revit-gp",
    label: "Create Group (Revit GP)",
    keys: "gp",
    category: "tool",
    handler: () => {
      const ids = selIds();
      if (!ids.length) {
        notify("info", "Select elements to group");
        return;
      }
      const name = window.prompt("Group name:", "Group") ?? "Group";
      (window as Any).__obsGroups?.createFromSelection?.(name, ids, true);
      notify("success", `Group "${name}"`);
    },
  });

  // -- Architecture extras (Revit-like) -----------------------------------
  const tool = (keys: string, id: string, label: string, type: string) => {
    reg({
      id,
      label,
      keys,
      category: "tool",
      handler: () => {
        const a = getAuthoring();
        if (a?.setAuthoringTool) {
          a.setAuthoringTool(type);
          notify("info", label);
        } else {
          notify("warn", "Authoring not ready");
        }
      },
    });
  };
  // Already in default-shortcuts: WA SL CM BM DR WN GR LV ...
  // Add a few more Revit-common ones if missing from defaults
  tool("rf", "revit-tool-roof", "Roof (Revit RF)", "roof");
  tool("cl", "revit-tool-ceiling", "Ceiling (Revit CL)", "ceiling");
  tool("rm", "revit-tool-room", "Room (Revit RM)", "room");
  tool("st", "revit-tool-stairs", "Stairs (Revit ST)", "stairs");
  tool("ra", "revit-tool-railing", "Railing (Revit RA)", "railing");
  tool("cw", "revit-tool-curtain", "Curtain Wall (Revit CW)", "curtain-wall");

  // -- Help overlay -------------------------------------------------------
  reg({
    id: "revit-help-input",
    label: "Mouse & Keyboard Help (Revit map)",
    keys: "ctrl+/",
    category: "app",
    handler: () => toggleInputHelp(),
  });

  console.log("[revit-input-parity] Revit shortcuts registered");
}

/* -- Mouse / CameraControls guard ------------------------------------------ */

function enforceNavOwnership(): void {
  try {
    const controls = (ctx as Any).world?.camera?.controls;
    if (!controls) return;
    const ctor: Any = (controls as Any).constructor;
    const ACTION = (controls as Any).ACTION ?? (ctor && ctor.ACTION);
    const mb = controls.mouseButtons;
    if (mb && ACTION) {
      // Force CameraControls mouse off - navigation.ts is sole driver
      if (ACTION.NONE != null) {
        mb.left = ACTION.NONE;
        mb.middle = ACTION.NONE;
        mb.right = ACTION.NONE;
        mb.wheel = ACTION.NONE;
      }
    }
  } catch { /* */ }
}

export function openInputHelp(): void {
  installHelpStyle();
  if (!helpEl) {
    helpEl = document.createElement("div");
    helpEl.id = "obs-input-help";
    helpEl.innerHTML = buildHelpHtml();
    document.body.appendChild(helpEl);
    helpEl.querySelector(".ih-close")?.addEventListener("click", () => closeInputHelp());
    helpEl.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      if (e.target === helpEl) closeInputHelp();
    });
  }
  helpEl.classList.add("is-open");
}

export function closeInputHelp(): void {
  helpEl?.classList.remove("is-open");
}

export function toggleInputHelp(): void {
  if (helpEl?.classList.contains("is-open")) closeInputHelp();
  else openInputHelp();
}

/* -- Setup ----------------------------------------------------------------- */

export function setupRevitInputParity(): void {
  void registerRevitShortcuts();

  // Re-assert nav ownership after boot (CameraControls can re-enable)
  enforceNavOwnership();
  window.setInterval(enforceNavOwnership, 4000);

  // Esc closes help
  window.addEventListener(
    "keydown",
    (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && helpEl?.classList.contains("is-open")) {
        closeInputHelp();
      }
    },
    true,
  );

  (window as Any).__obsRevitInput = {
    openHelp: openInputHelp,
    closeHelp: closeInputHelp,
    toggleHelp: toggleInputHelp,
    enforceNav: enforceNavOwnership,
  };

  // Command palette
  void (async () => {
    try {
      const cp: Any = await import("./command-palette");
      cp.registerCommand?.({
        id: "parity.inputHelp",
        label: "Mouse & Keyboard Help (Revit map)",
        category: "View",
        kbd: "Ctrl+/",
        run: () => toggleInputHelp(),
      });
    } catch { /* */ }
  })();

  console.log("[revit-input-parity] ready - Ctrl+/ for mouse & keyboard map");
}


===== FILE: src/features/revit-parity-bridge.ts =====
/**
 * Revit Parity Bridge - single integration layer that:
 *   1. Registers palette / toolbox commands for every gap-closure module
 *   2. Places stairs, railings, curtain walls into the live THREE scene
 *   3. Hooks selection - temporary dimensions
 *   4. Applies visibility filter on authoring rebuild events
 *   5. Exposes Match Properties + Create Similar helpers
 *
 * Call setupRevitParityBridge() from critical-gaps-boot (already wired).
 */

import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { notify } from "../core/notify";

type Any = Record<string, any>;

function scene(): THREE.Scene | null {
  return (ctx as Any).world?.scene?.three ?? null;
}

function getAuthoring(): Any | null {
  return (ctx.features as Any)?.authoring ?? (window as Any).__obsAuthoring ?? null;
}

function getSelectionIds(): string[] {
  const sel = (ctx.features as Any)?.selection;
  try {
    if (sel?.getSelectedIds) return sel.getSelectedIds() ?? [];
    if (sel?.ids) return [...sel.ids()];
    if (sel?.size && sel.size() && sel.get) {
      // fallback
    }
  } catch { /* */ }
  return [];
}

function getElements(): Any[] {
  const a = getAuthoring();
  if (!a) return [];
  try {
    if (typeof a.getElements === "function") return a.getElements() ?? [];
    if (Array.isArray(a.elements)) return a.elements;
  } catch { /* */ }
  return [];
}

function rebuild(): void {
  const a = getAuthoring();
  if (!a) return;
  try {
    if (typeof a.rebuildAuthored === "function") a.rebuildAuthored();
    else if (typeof a.rebuild === "function") a.rebuild();
    if (typeof a.markAuthoringDirty === "function") a.markAuthoringDirty();
  } catch { /* */ }
}

/* -- Geometry placement helpers ------------------------------------------ */

const placedGroupName = "obs-parity-placed";

function placedRoot(): THREE.Group | null {
  const s = scene();
  if (!s) return null;
  let g = s.getObjectByName(placedGroupName) as THREE.Group | undefined;
  if (!g) {
    g = new THREE.Group();
    g.name = placedGroupName;
    s.add(g);
  }
  return g;
}

function placeObject(obj: THREE.Object3D, label: string): void {
  const root = placedRoot();
  if (!root) {
    notify("warn", "Scene not ready");
    return;
  }
  obj.userData.parityPlaced = true;
  obj.userData.label = label;
  root.add(obj);
  notify("success", `Placed ${label}`, { timeout: 1600 });
}

async function placeStair(runType: "straight" | "l-shape" | "u-shape" = "straight"): Promise<void> {
  const m = await import("../modules/stairs/stairs");
  const result = m.buildStair({
    totalRise: 3.0,
    width: 1.2,
    runType,
    origin: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
  });
  const comfort = m.stairComfort(result.riserHeight, 0.28);
  placeObject(result.object, `Stair (${runType}, ${result.riserCount} risers)`);
  if (!comfort.ok) notify("warn", comfort.message, { timeout: 3000 });
}

async function placeRailing(): Promise<void> {
  const m = await import("../modules/railings/railings");
  const result = m.buildRailing({
    path: [
      { x: 0, z: 0 },
      { x: 4, z: 0 },
      { x: 4, z: 3 },
    ],
    height: 1.1,
    balusterSpacing: 0.12,
  });
  placeObject(result.object, `Railing (${result.balusterCount} balusters)`);
}

async function placeCurtainWall(): Promise<void> {
  const m = await import("../modules/curtain-wall/curtain-wall");
  const result = m.buildCurtainWall({
    width: 6,
    height: 3.5,
    vDivisions: 4,
    hDivisions: 2,
    origin: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
  });
  placeObject(result.object, `Curtain wall (${result.panelCount} panels)`);
}

/* -- Match Properties ---------------------------------------------------- */

let matchSourceId: string | null = null;

function matchPropertiesPickSource(): void {
  const ids = getSelectionIds();
  if (ids.length !== 1) {
    notify("info", "Select exactly one element as the property source");
    return;
  }
  matchSourceId = ids[0];
  notify("info", "Match Properties: source set - select targets and run Apply", { timeout: 2500 });
}

function matchPropertiesApply(): number {
  if (!matchSourceId) {
    notify("warn", "No source - run Match Properties (Pick Source) first");
    return 0;
  }
  const elements = getElements();
  const source = elements.find((e) => e.id === matchSourceId);
  if (!source) {
    notify("warn", "Source element no longer exists");
    matchSourceId = null;
    return 0;
  }
  const targets = getSelectionIds().filter((id) => id !== matchSourceId);
  if (!targets.length) {
    notify("info", "Select target elements to paint properties onto");
    return 0;
  }

  // Copy type-level visual / identity properties (not geometry params)
  const paintKeys = ["color", "familyId", "familyTypeId"];
  const paintProps = source.properties ? { ...source.properties } : {};
  let n = 0;
  for (const id of targets) {
    const el = elements.find((e) => e.id === id);
    if (!el) continue;
    for (const k of paintKeys) {
      if (k in source) (el as Any)[k] = (source as Any)[k];
    }
    el.properties = { ...(el.properties ?? {}), ...paintProps };
    // Phase / design option
    if ((source as Any).phaseCreated != null) (el as Any).phaseCreated = (source as Any).phaseCreated;
    if ((source as Any).designOptionId != null) (el as Any).designOptionId = (source as Any).designOptionId;
    n++;
  }
  rebuild();
  notify("success", `Matched properties onto ${n} element(s)`, { timeout: 1800 });
  return n;
}

/* -- Create Similar ------------------------------------------------------ */

function createSimilar(): void {
  const a = getAuthoring();
  if (!a) return;
  if (typeof a.createSimilarFromSelection === "function") {
    a.createSimilarFromSelection();
    return;
  }
  const ids = getSelectionIds();
  if (ids.length !== 1) {
    notify("info", "Select one element to create similar");
    return;
  }
  const el = getElements().find((e) => e.id === ids[0]);
  if (!el) return;
  if (typeof a.setAuthoringTool === "function") {
    a.setAuthoringTool(el.type);
    if (el.familyId && typeof a.setActiveFamily === "function") {
      a.setActiveFamily(el.familyId, el.familyTypeId ?? null);
    }
    notify("success", `Tool: ${el.type} (similar)`, { timeout: 1600 });
  }
}

/* -- Selection - temp dims ----------------------------------------------- */

function hookTempDims(): void {
  const sel = (ctx.features as Any)?.selection;
  if (!sel?.onChange) return;
  sel.onChange(() => {
    const td = (ctx.features as Any)?.tempDimensions ?? (window as Any).__obsTempDims;
    if (!td?.autoForElement) return;
    const ids = getSelectionIds();
    if (ids.length !== 1) {
      td.clear?.();
      return;
    }
    const el = getElements().find((e) => e.id === ids[0]);
    if (el) td.autoForElement(el);
  });
}

/* -- Visibility on authoring-changed ------------------------------------- */

function hookVisibility(): void {
  window.addEventListener("obs:authoring-changed", () => {
    const vis = (window as Any).__obsVisibility;
    if (!vis?.isVisible) return;
    // Soft filter: mark userData; full mesh hide is left to authoring rebuild consumers
    const elements = getElements();
    for (const el of elements) {
      const visible = vis.isVisible({
        id: el.id,
        category: el.type,
        phaseCreated: (el as Any).phaseCreated ?? el.properties?.phaseCreated,
        phaseDemolished: (el as Any).phaseDemolished ?? el.properties?.phaseDemolished,
        designOptionId: (el as Any).designOptionId ?? el.properties?.designOptionId,
      });
      (el as Any)._visHidden = !visible;
    }
  });
}

/* -- Setup --------------------------------------------------------------- */

export function setupRevitParityBridge(): void {
  hookTempDims();
  hookVisibility();
  void registerCommands();

  (ctx.features as Any).revitParity = {
    placeStair,
    placeRailing,
    placeCurtainWall,
    matchPropertiesPickSource,
    matchPropertiesApply,
    createSimilar,
  };
  (window as Any).__obsRevitParity = (ctx.features as Any).revitParity;

  console.log("[revit-parity-bridge] ready");
}

/**
 * Parametric ribbon tab + parity tools.
 * Adds a top-level "Parametric" tab to .obs-ribbon with:
 *   Families | Constraints | Architecture generators | CAD 2D | Drawings DXF/DWG
 * Hides bottom parity bars.
 */

type Any = Record<string, any>;

let installed = false;
let stripObserver: MutationObserver | null = null;
let parametricActive = false;
let savedStrip: Node[] | null = null;

function hideBottomBars(): void {
  const hide = (el: Element | null) => {
    if (!el) return;
    const h = el as HTMLElement;
    h.style.display = "none";
    h.setAttribute("data-parity-ribbon-migrated", "1");
  };
  hide(document.getElementById("obs-parity-tabs"));
  hide(document.getElementById("obs-adv-toolbar"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".obs-adv-toolbar").forEach(hide);
  document.querySelectorAll("[id^=obs-][id$=-launch]").forEach(hide);
}

function clickAdv(id: string): void {
  document.getElementById(id)?.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
}

function esc(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) =>
    ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" } as Any)[c],
  );
}

function getIconForLabel(label: string): string {
  const l = label.toLowerCase();
  if (l.includes("family")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.family;
  if (l.includes("door")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.door;
  if (l.includes("solve")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.solve;
  if (l.includes("host")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.host;
  if (l.includes("stair")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.stair;
  if (l.includes("railing")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.railing;
  if (l.includes("curtain")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.curtain;
  if (l.includes("rooms") && l.includes("panel")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.rooms;
  if (l === "room" || l === "rooms") return OBS_ICONS_SVG.room;
  if (l.includes("phase")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.phase;
  if (l.includes("option")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.option;
  if (l.includes("group")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.group;
  if (l.includes("align")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.align;
  if (l.includes("cad tool")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.cad;
  if (l.includes("pline")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.pline;
  if (l.includes("line")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.line;
  if (l.includes("rect")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.rect;
  if (l.includes("circle")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.circle;
  if (l.includes("offset")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.offset;
  if (l.includes("trim")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.trim;
  if (l.includes("extend")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.extend;
  if (l.includes("fillet")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.fillet;
  if (l.includes("mirror")) return OBS_ICONS_SVG.mirror;
  return OBS_ICONS_SVG.dot;
}

function injectParametricCSS(): void {
  if (document.getElementById("obs-parametric-css")) return;
  const s = document.createElement("style");
  s.id = "obs-parametric-css";
  s.textContent = [
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container {",
    "  display: grid !important;",
    "  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(56px, 1fr)) !important;",
    "  grid-auto-rows: auto !important;",
    "  gap: 2px !important;",
    "  padding: 4px !important;",
    "  overflow-x: auto !important;",
    "  overflow-y: hidden !important;",
    "  max-height: 104px !important;",
    "  align-items: stretch !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb {",
    "  min-width: 52px !important;",
    "  padding: 2px !important;",
    "  height: auto !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb__icon {",
    "  display: flex !important;",
    "  align-items: center !important;",
    "  justify-content: center !important;",
    "  width: 100% !important;",
    "  height: 20px !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb__icon svg {",
    "  width: 16px !important;",
    "  height: 16px !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb__label {",
    "  font-size: 9px !important;",
    "  line-height: 1.1 !important;",
    "  text-align: center !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb-sep {",
    "  display: none !important;",
    "}",
    ".obs-parametric-grid-container .obs-rb-group {",
    "  grid-column: 1 / -1 !important;",
    "  font-size: 8px !important;",
    "  padding: 4px 2px 2px !important;",
    "  border-bottom: 1px solid #2a2e3a !important;",
    "  margin-bottom: 2px !important;",
    "  align-self: center !important;",
    "}",
  ].join("\n");
  document.head.appendChild(s);
}

function applyParametricGridLayout(): void {
  injectParametricCSS();
  const firstBtn = document.querySelector('[data-parametric="1"].obs-rb') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!firstBtn) return;
  let parent: HTMLElement | null = firstBtn.parentElement;
  let depth = 0;
  while (parent && depth < 5) {
    const count = parent.querySelectorAll('[data-parametric="1"]').length;
    if (count > 3) {
      parent.classList.add("obs-parametric-grid-container");
      return;
    }
    parent = parent.parentElement;
    depth++;
  }
}

function makeBtn(label: string, title: string, onClick: () => void): HTMLButtonElement {
  const el = document.createElement("button");
  el.className = "obs-rb";
  el.type = "button";
  el.title = title || label;
  el.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
  el.dataset.parityInject = "1";
  el.dataset.parametric = "1";
  const iconSvg = getIconForLabel(label);
  el.innerHTML =
    `<span class="obs-rb__icon">${iconSvg}</span>` +
    `<span class="obs-rb__label">${esc(label)}</span>`;
  el.onclick = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    try {
      onClick();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[parametric-tab]", err);
    }
  };
  return el;
}

// Retry attaching the grid layout for 12 s after install (buttons render on tab activation)
(function autoApplyParametricLayout() {
  let tries = 0;
  const iv = setInterval(() => {
    tries++;
    try { applyParametricGridLayout(); } catch { /* */ }
    if (document.querySelector(".obs-parametric-grid-container") || tries > 30) {
      clearInterval(iv);
    }
  }, 400);
})();

function sep(): HTMLElement {
  const d = document.createElement("div");
  d.className = "obs-rb-sep";
  d.dataset.parityInject = "1";
  d.dataset.parametric = "1";
  d.style.cssText = "width:1px;align-self:stretch;background:#2a2e3a;margin:4px 6px";
  return d;
}

function groupLabel(text: string): HTMLElement {
  const s = document.createElement("span");
  s.className = "obs-rb-group";
  s.dataset.parityInject = "1";
  s.dataset.parametric = "1";
  s.textContent = text;
  s.style.cssText =
    "font:700 9px/1 Inter,system-ui;letter-spacing:.5px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#6b7280;padding:0 4px;align-self:center";
  return s;
}
