function ensureParametricTab(): HTMLButtonElement | null {
  const tabsRow = document.querySelector(".obs-ribbon__tabs") as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!tabsRow) return null;

  let tab = tabsRow.querySelector('[data-tab="parametric"]') as HTMLButtonElement | null;
  if (tab) return tab;

  tab = document.createElement("button");
  tab.type = "button";
  tab.className = "obs-ribbon__tab";
  tab.dataset.tab = "parametric";
  tab.textContent = "Parametric";
  tab.title = "Parametric families, CAD 2D, Drawings DXF/DWG";
  tab.style.cssText = ""; // inherit ribbon tab styles

  // Place after Architecture if present, else append
  const arch = Array.from(tabsRow.querySelectorAll("button")).find((b) =>
    /architecture/i.test(b.textContent || ""),
  );
  if (arch && arch.nextSibling) {
    tabsRow.insertBefore(tab, arch.nextSibling);
  } else {
    tabsRow.appendChild(tab);
  }

  tab.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    activateParametricTab();
  });

  return tab;
}

function clearActiveTabs(): void {
  document.querySelectorAll(".obs-ribbon__tabs button").forEach((b) => {
    const el = b as HTMLElement;
    el.removeAttribute("data-active");
    el.dataset.active = "";
    el.classList.remove("is-active", "active");
  });
}

/**
 * Wrap the flat array from parametricTools() into the same group structure
 * the Architecture tab uses: each group gets a .obs-ribbon-group container,
 * with buttons inside .obs-ribbon-group__body and the group name in
 * .obs-ribbon-group__label at the bottom. This gives the 2-row wrap and the
 * centred label that the Architecture tab has.
 */
function buildGroupedRibbon(flat: HTMLElement[]): HTMLElement[] {
  const grouped: HTMLElement[] = [];
  let current: HTMLElement | null = null;
  let body: HTMLElement | null = null;
  let pendingLabel: string = "";

  const flush = () => {
    if (!current || !body) return;
    if (body.children.length === 0) return;
    const lbl = document.createElement("div");
    lbl.className = "obs-ribbon-group__label";
    lbl.textContent = pendingLabel || "";
    lbl.style.cssText = "text-transform:uppercase;font:700 9px/1 Inter,system-ui;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#6b7280;text-align:center;padding:2px 0 4px";
    current.appendChild(body);
    current.appendChild(lbl);
    grouped.push(current);
  };

  for (const item of flat) {
    if (item.classList.contains("obs-rb-group")) {
      flush();
      current = document.createElement("div");
      current.className = "obs-ribbon-group";
      body = document.createElement("div");
      body.className = "obs-ribbon-group__body";
      pendingLabel = (item.textContent || "").trim();
    } else if (item.classList.contains("obs-rb-sep")) {
      // separators are redundant once grouped â€” group borders handle spacing
    } else if (body) {
      body.appendChild(item);
    }
  }
  flush();

  return grouped.length ? grouped : flat;
}
function activateParametricTab(): void {
  parametricActive = true;
  clearActiveTabs();
  const tab = ensureParametricTab();
  if (tab) {
    tab.dataset.active = "1";
    tab.setAttribute("data-active", "1");
    tab.classList.add("is-active");
  }

  const strip = document.querySelector(".obs-ribbon__strip") as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!strip) return;

  // Clear strip and show parametric tools only
  strip.innerHTML = "";
  for (const el of buildGroupedRibbon(parametricTools())) strip.appendChild(el);
}

function deactivateParametricIfOtherTab(e: Event): void {
  const t = e.target as HTMLElement;
  if (!t) return;
  const btn = t.closest?.(".obs-ribbon__tabs button") as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!btn) return;
  if (btn.getAttribute("data-tab") === "parametric") return;
  // Other tab clicked - leave parametric mode; ribbon will re-render its strip
  parametricActive = false;
  const tab = document.querySelector('.obs-ribbon__tabs [data-tab="parametric"]') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (tab) {
    tab.removeAttribute("data-active");
    tab.dataset.active = "";
    tab.classList.remove("is-active");
  }
}

function inject(): void {
  hideBottomBars();
  ensureParametricTab();

  if (parametricActive) {
    activateParametricTab();
    return;
  }

  // Do not inject parity tools into other tabs anymore - they live under Parametric
  const strip = document.querySelector(".obs-ribbon__strip") as HTMLElement | null;
  if (strip) {
    strip.querySelectorAll("[data-parity-inject]").forEach((n) => n.remove());
  }
}

function watchRibbon(): void {
  const root = document.querySelector(".obs-ribbon") as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!root) {
    window.setTimeout(watchRibbon, 500);
    return;
  }

  root.addEventListener("click", deactivateParametricIfOtherTab, true);
  root.addEventListener(
    "click",
    () => {
      window.setTimeout(inject, 40);
      window.setTimeout(inject, 150);
    },
    true,
  );

  const strip = root.querySelector(".obs-ribbon__strip");
  if (strip && !stripObserver) {
    stripObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (parametricActive) {
        // Ribbon may wipe strip on re-render - restore parametric tools
        if (!strip.querySelector("[data-parametric]")) {
          window.setTimeout(() => activateParametricTab(), 20);
        }
      }
    });
    stripObserver.observe(strip, { childList: true });
  }

  inject();
  window.setTimeout(inject, 600);
  window.setTimeout(inject, 1800);
}

export function setupRibbonParityInject(): void {
  if (installed) return;
  installed = true;

  hideBottomBars();
  watchRibbon();

  const mo = new MutationObserver(() => hideBottomBars());
  mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  (window as Any).__obsRibbonParity = {
    inject,
    hideBottomBars,
    openParametric: activateParametricTab,
  };
  (window as Any).__obsParametricTab = { open: activateParametricTab };

  console.log(
    "[ribbon-parity-inject] Parametric tab ready (families + CAD 2D + DXF/DWG)",
  );
}


===== FILE: src/commands/CommandBase.ts =====
export abstract class Command {
  /** Human readable name */
  abstract name: string;
  /** Execute the command */
  abstract execute(): Promise<any>;
  /** Undo the command */
  abstract undo(): Promise<any>;
  /** Optional JSON serialization for versioning */
  toJSON?(): any {
    return { type: this.constructor.name };
  }
}


===== FILE: src/commands/CreateWall.ts =====
import { Command } from './CommandBase';
import { IfcAPI } from 'web-ifc';

export interface WallParams {
  start: [number, number, number];
  end: [number, number, number];
  height?: number;
  thickness?: number;
  fireRating?: string;
}

export class CreateWall extends Command {
  name = 'Create Wall';
  private wallId!: number;

  constructor(private api?: IfcAPI, private params?: WallParams) {
    super();
  }

  async execute() {
    if (!this.api || !this.params) throw new Error('IfcAPI or parameters not provided');
    const { start, end, height = 3000, thickness = 200, fireRating } = this.params;
    const profile = await this.api.CreateRectangleProfileDef({ XDim: thickness, YDim: height });
    // direction vector length
    const dir = [end[0] - start[0], end[1] - start[1], end[2] - start[2]] as [number, number, number];
    const length = Math.hypot(...dir);
    const placement = await this.api.CreateAxis2Placement3D({ Location: start });
    const solid = await this.api.CreateExtrudedAreaSolid({
      SweptArea: profile,
      Depth: length,
      ExtrudedDirection: [0, 0, 1],
      Position: placement,
    });
    const wall = await this.api.CreateIfcWallStandardCase({
      ObjectPlacement: placement,
      Representation: {
        Representations: [
          {
            RepresentationIdentifier: 'Body',
            RepresentationType: 'SweptSolid',
            Items: [solid],
          },
        ],
      },
    });
    if (fireRating) {
      const pset = await this.api.CreatePropertySet('Pset_FireRating', [
        { Name: 'FireRating', NominalValue: fireRating },
      ]);
      await this.api.AssignPropertySet(wall, pset);
    }
    this.wallId = wall.id;
    return this.wallId;
  }

  async undo() {
    await this.api!.DeleteEntity!(this.wallId);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/commands/CreateStairAdvanced.ts =====
// src/commands/CreateStairAdvanced.ts
import { Command } from './CommandBase';
import { IfcAPI } from 'web-ifc';
import * as THREE from 'three';

/**
 * Advanced stair generator that creates separate tread and riser elements.
 * For demo purposes we only create a simple monolithic geometry but expose
 * a clear extension point for generating multiple IFC elements.
 */
export class CreateStairAdvanced extends Command {
  name = 'Create Advanced Stair';
  private stairIds: number[] = [];
  constructor(private api?: IfcAPI, private params?: {
    base: [number, number, number];
    direction: [number, number, number];
    rise: number;
    run: number;
    treadDepth: number;
    treadWidth: number;
    numSteps: number;
  }) { super(); }

  async execute() {
    if (!this.api || !this.params) throw new Error('IfcAPI or parameters not provided');
    const { base, direction, rise, run, treadDepth, treadWidth, numSteps } = this.params!;
    for (let i = 0; i < numSteps; i++) {
      const stepGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(treadWidth, rise, treadDepth);
      const placement = await this.api.CreateAxis2Placement3D({
        Location: [base[0] + direction[0] * (i * treadDepth), base[1] + i * rise, base[2]],
        // orientation omitted for brevity
      });
      const shape = await this.api.CreateIfcShapeRepresentation({
        ContextOfItems: 0,
        RepresentationIdentifier: 'Body',
        RepresentationType: 'SweptSolid',
        Items: [{ type: 'IfcFacetedBrep', geometry: stepGeometry }],
      });
      const elem = await this.api.CreateIfcBuildingElementProxy({ ObjectPlacement: placement, Representation: shape });
      this.stairIds.push(elem.id);
    }
    return this.stairIds;
  }

  async undo() {
    for (const id of this.stairIds) {
      await this.api!.DeleteEntity!(id);
    }
  }
}


===== FILE: src/modules/revit-gaps/revit-gaps-boot.ts =====
/**
 * Revit-gaps boot (Milestones 1, 2 and 3).
 *
 * Wires, in order: per-project store persistence, real visibility (authored +
 * Fragments), wall-bounded rooms, the M2 element types (stairs, railings, ramps,
 * curtain walls, roofs, ceilings) and the authored-model IFC export. Each step
 * is isolated; the summary printed at the end lists what actually started and
 * what failed (the older boot logged "all wired" regardless).
 */
import { ctx } from "../../core/context";
import { notify } from "../../core/notify";
import { roomsStore } from "../rooms/rooms";
import { exportAuthoredToIfc, type IfcExportOptions, type IfcSchema } from "./authored-ifc";
import { setupProjectScope } from "./project-scope";
import {
  setupVisibilityBridge, hideSelection, isolateSelection, resetTemporaryVisibility,
  promptAssignPhase, promptAssignDesignOption, tagSelection, getSelectedKeys, applyAuthored,
} from "./visibility-bridge";
import { setupRoomTool, startRoomTool, placeRoomAt, placeRoomsAutomatically, refreshRoomOverlay } from "./room-tool";
import { setupM2Register } from "./m2-register";
import { installM2Redirects, startM2Tool, ceilingsFromRooms, createM2Element } from "./m2-tools";
import { setupM3, togglePlanOption, refreshViews } from "./m3-register";

type Any = Record<string, any>;
export interface StepStatus { name: string; ok: boolean; detail: string }
export const REVIT_GAPS_VERSION = "1.2.0-m3";
const status: StepStatus[] = [];

const authoring = (): Any | null => ((ctx.features as Any).authoring ?? (window as Any).__obsAuthoring ?? null);

/** Build the IFC text for the current authored model + rooms. */
export async function buildModelIfc(schema: IfcSchema = "IFC4") {
  const a = authoring();
  if (!a) throw new Error("Authoring is not ready");
  let project: Any | null = null;
  try { project = (await import("../projects/projects")).getCurrentProject(); } catch { /* */ }
  const name: string = project?.name || "OpenBIMStudio";
  const spaces = roomsStore.listRooms().map((r) => ({
    id: r.id, name: r.name, number: r.number, levelId: r.levelId, boundary: r.boundary,
    holes: (r.properties?.holes as Array<Array<{ x: number; z: number }>> | undefined) ?? [],
    height: r.height, area: r.area, department: r.department,
  }));
  const opts: IfcExportOptions = {
    schema, projectName: name, projectId: project?.id, levels: a.getLevels?.() ?? [], spaces,
    fileName: `${name.replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, "_")}_${schema}.ifc`,
  };
  return { opts, ...exportAuthoredToIfc(a.getElements?.() ?? [], opts) };
}

/** Export the authored model to an .ifc download. */
export async function exportIfc(schema: IfcSchema = "IFC4"): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const r = await buildModelIfc(schema);
    if (!r.stats.elements && !r.stats.spaces) { notify("warn", "Nothing to export - the authored model is empty"); return; }
    const blob = new Blob([r.text], { type: "application/x-step" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = Object.assign(document.createElement("a"), { href: url, download: r.opts.fileName });
    document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); link.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 5000);
    const skipped = r.stats.skipped.length;
    notify(skipped ? "warn" : "success",
      `${schema}: ${r.stats.elements} element(s), ${r.stats.spaces} room(s), ${r.stats.openings} opening(s)` + (skipped ? ` - ${skipped} not exported (see console)` : ""), { timeout: 4500 });
    if (skipped) console.table(r.stats.skipped);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[revit-gaps] IFC export failed", e);
    notify("error", "IFC export failed: " + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
  }
}

async function step(name: string, fn: () => unknown): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const r: any = await fn();
    status.push({ name, ok: r?.ok !== false, detail: r?.detail ?? "started" });
  } catch (e) {
    status.push({ name, ok: false, detail: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
    console.error(`[revit-gaps] ${name} failed`, e);
  }
}

let booted = false;
export async function setupRevitGaps(): Promise<StepStatus[]> {
  if (booted) return status;
  booted = true;

  await step("project-scope", setupProjectScope);
  await step("visibility-bridge", setupVisibilityBridge);
  await step("room-tool", setupRoomTool);
  await step("m2-elements", () => {
    const r = setupM2Register();
    installM2Redirects();
    return { ok: r.ok, detail: r.detail + "; ST/RA/CW/RF/CL tools and parity buttons redirected" };
  });
  await step("m3-views", setupM3);
  await step("ifc-export", () => ({ ok: true, detail: "File > Export > Model > IFC4 / IFC2X3" }));
  await step("commands", async () => {
    const { registerCommand } = await import("../../features/command-palette");
    const cmds: Array<[string, string, string, () => unknown]> = [
      ["rg-room-place", "Room: place (click inside walls)", "Rooms", startRoomTool],
      ["rg-room-auto", "Room: place rooms automatically", "Rooms", () => placeRoomsAutomatically()],
      ["rg-stairs", "Stairs: straight run", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("stairs", "straight")],
      ["rg-stairs-l", "Stairs: L-shape (quarter turn)", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("stairs", "L")],
      ["rg-stairs-u", "Stairs: U-shape (half turn)", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("stairs", "U")],
      ["rg-railing", "Railing: draw path", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("railing")],
      ["rg-ramp", "Ramp: place", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("ramp")],
      ["rg-curtain-wall", "Curtain wall: place", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("curtain-wall")],
      ["rg-roof", "Roof: draw footprint (gable / shed)", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("roof")],
      ["rg-ceiling", "Ceiling: draw outline", "Architecture", () => startM2Tool("ceiling")],
      ["rg-ceilings-rooms", "Ceiling: place one in every room of this level", "Architecture", () => ceilingsFromRooms()],
      ["rg-pl-poche", "Plan graphics: toggle wall poche", "Documentation", () => togglePlanOption("poche", "Wall poche")],
      ["rg-pl-rooms", "Plan graphics: toggle room tags", "Documentation", () => togglePlanOption("roomTags", "Room tags")],
      ["rg-pl-roomlines", "Plan graphics: toggle room boundaries", "Documentation", () => togglePlanOption("roomBoundaries", "Room boundaries")],
      ["rg-pl-doors", "Plan graphics: toggle door and window marks", "Documentation", () => togglePlanOption("doorTags", "Door/window marks")],
      ["rg-pl-stairs", "Plan graphics: toggle stair symbols", "Documentation", () => togglePlanOption("stairSymbols", "Stair symbols")],
      ["rg-views-refresh", "Views: regenerate all views and sheets", "Documentation", () => { void refreshViews(); }],
      ["rg-hide", "Visibility: hide selection", "Visibility", hideSelection],
      ["rg-isolate", "Visibility: isolate selection", "Visibility", isolateSelection],
      ["rg-reset-vis", "Visibility: reset temporary hide/isolate", "Visibility", resetTemporaryVisibility],
      ["rg-phase-created", "Phase: set 'phase created' on selection", "Phasing", () => promptAssignPhase("created")],
      ["rg-phase-demolished", "Phase: set 'phase demolished' on selection", "Phasing", () => promptAssignPhase("demolished")],
      ["rg-design-option", "Design option: assign selection", "Phasing", promptAssignDesignOption],
      ["rg-export-ifc4", "Export: model to IFC4", "Export", () => exportIfc("IFC4")],
      ["rg-export-ifc2x3", "Export: model to IFC2X3 (Revit 2020)", "Export", () => exportIfc("IFC2X3")],
      ["rg-status", "Revit gaps: show status", "Help", () => { console.table(status); notify("info", status.map((s) => `${s.ok ? "OK" : "FAILED"} ${s.name}`).join(" | "), { timeout: 6000 }); }],
    ];
    for (const [id, label, category, run] of cmds) registerCommand({ id, label, category, run });
    return { ok: true, detail: `${cmds.length} commands` };
  });

  (window as Any).__obsRevitGaps = {
    version: REVIT_GAPS_VERSION, status,
    exportIfc, buildModelIfc,
    placeRoomAt, placeRoomsAutomatically, startRoomTool, refreshRoomOverlay,
    m2: { start: startM2Tool, create: createM2Element, ceilingsFromRooms },
    visibility: { hideSelection, isolateSelection, reset: resetTemporaryVisibility, tagSelection, getSelectedKeys, apply: () => applyAuthored(true) },
  };
  const failed = status.filter((s) => !s.ok);
  console.log(`[revit-gaps] ${REVIT_GAPS_VERSION}: ${status.length - failed.length}/${status.length} steps started` + (failed.length ? ` - FAILED: ${failed.map((f) => `${f.name} (${f.detail})`).join("; ")}` : ""));
  if (failed.length) notify("warn", `Revit gaps: ${failed.length} step(s) failed - see console`);
  return status;
}


===== FILE: src/modules/revit-gaps/m2-register.ts =====
/**
 * Milestone 2 registration: makes stairs, railings, ramps, curtain walls, roofs
 * and ceilings first-class authored elements.
 *
 *  - mesh builders: authoring.createMesh() asks window.__obsMeshBuilders[type]
 *    (one anchored line added by the installer) before giving up on a type;
 *  - Properties palette: schemas for the six types (SCHEMAS is a mutable table);
 *  - quantities: window.__obsM2Quantities(el) feeds the cost takeoff (installer patch).
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../../core/context";
import { SCHEMAS, type ElementSchema, type PropertyField } from "../../features/property-schema";
import { buildElementGeo, m2Quantities, M2_TYPES, buildCompoundWall, parseWallLayers } from "./m2-geometry";

type Any = Record<string, any>;
const warned = new Set<string>();

export function buildM2Mesh(el: Any): THREE.Object3D | null {
  let geo;
  try { geo = buildElementGeo(el as never); }
  catch (e) {
    if (!warned.has(el.id)) { warned.add(el.id); console.warn(`[revit-gaps] ${el.type} ${el.id}: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : e}`); }
    return null;
  }
  if (!geo) return null;
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = `${el.type}:${el.id}`;
  for (const part of geo.parts) {
    if (part.positions.length < 9) continue;
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute("position", new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(part.positions, 3));
    g.computeVertexNormals();
    const glass = part.role === "glass";
    const mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(part.color), transparent: glass, opacity: glass ? 0.35 : 1, depthWrite: !glass });
    if (el.type === "roof" || el.type === "ceiling") mat.side = THREE.DoubleSide;
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat);
    mesh.userData.elementId = el.id;
    mesh.userData.baseColor = part.color;
    mesh.userData.m2Part = part.name;
    group.add(mesh);
  }
  return group.children.length ? group : null;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------- palette schemas */

const D = "Dimensions", C = "Constraints", I = "Identity Data", M = "Materials", K = "Construction";
const num = (key: string, label: string, group: string, step = 0.05, min?: number, max?: number, unit = "m"): PropertyField =>
  ({ key, label, group, type: "number", unit, step, min, max, target: "params" });
const prop = (key: string, label: string, group: string, step = 0.05, min?: number, max?: number, unit = "m", def?: number): PropertyField =>
  ({ key, label, group, type: "number", unit, step, min, max, target: "properties", default: def });
const sel = (key: string, label: string, group: string, options: Array<[string, string]>, def: string): PropertyField =>
  ({ key, label, group, type: "select", target: "properties", options: options.map(([value, l]) => ({ value, label: l })), default: def });
const common = (): PropertyField[] => [
  { key: "name", label: "Name", group: I, type: "text", target: "root" },
  { key: "typeMark", label: "Type mark", group: I, type: "text", target: "properties" },
  { key: "comments", label: "Comments", group: I, type: "text", target: "properties" },
  { key: "color", label: "Color", group: M, type: "color", target: "root" },
];
const elev = (label = "Base elevation") => num("elevation", label, C, 0.1);
const schema = (type: ElementSchema["type"], label: string, icon: string, fields: PropertyField[]): ElementSchema =>
  ({ type, label, icon, groups: [D, C, K, I, M], fields: [...fields, ...common()] });

export function registerM2Schemas(): void {

  S.stairs = schema("stairs" as never, "Stair", "stairs", [
    num("height", "Total rise", D, 0.05, 0.3), num("width", "Run width", D, 0.05, 0.5), elev(),
    sel("stairShape", "Shape", K, [["straight", "Straight run"], ["L", "L-shape (quarter turn)"], ["U", "U-shape (half turn)"]], "straight"),

    prop("maxRiser", "Max riser height", K, 0.005, 0.1, 0.25, "m", 0.175), prop("waist", "Waist thickness", K, 0.01, 0, 0.5, "m", 0.12),
    prop("run2", "Second flight length", K, 0.1, 0.5, undefined, "m"), prop("gap", "Gap between flights (U)", K, 0.05, 0, 1, "m", 0.1),
    prop("landingThickness", "Landing thickness", K, 0.01, 0.05, 0.5, "m", 0.15),
  ]);
  S.railing = schema("railing" as never, "Railing", "railing", [

    prop("rise", "Rise along path", C, 0.05, 0, undefined, "m", 0), prop("postSpacing", "Max post spacing", K, 0.1, 0.3, 4, "m", 1.2),
    prop("balusterSpacing", "Baluster spacing", K, 0.01, 0.04, 0.5, "m", 0.12),

    num("height", "Rise", D, 0.05, 0.05), num("width", "Width", D, 0.05, 0.5), num("thickness", "Slab thickness", D, 0.01, 0.05), elev(),
  ]);
