/* ------------------------------------------------------------------- setup */

let installed = false;
export function setupM2Register(): { ok: boolean; detail: string } {
  if (installed) return { ok: true, detail: "already installed" };
  installed = true;
  const w = window as Any;
    w.__obsMeshBuilders = w.__obsMeshBuilders ?? {};
  for (const t of M2_TYPES) w.__obsMeshBuilders[t] = buildM2Mesh;

  // Register Compound Wall builder
  w.__obsMeshBuilders["wall"] = (el: Any) => {
    const p = el.params || {};
    const layerStr = el.properties?.layers;
    if (layerStr) {
      const layers = parseWallLayers(layerStr, Number(p.thickness) || 0.2);
      const geo = buildCompoundWall(p, layers, el.color || "#d9d4c7");
      if (!geo) return null;

      const group = new THREE.Group();
      group.name = `wall:${el.id}`;
      for (const part of geo.parts) {
        const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        g.setAttribute("position", new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(part.positions, 3));
        g.computeVertexNormals();
        const mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(part.color) });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat);
        mesh.userData.elementId = el.id;
        mesh.userData.baseColor = part.color;
        group.add(mesh);
      }
      return group;
    }
    return null;
  };
  w.__obsM2Quantities = (el: Any) => m2Quantities(el as never);
  registerM2Schemas();
  // authored data may already contain these types: rebuild once so they appear
  try { (ctx.features as Any).authoring?.rebuildAuthored?.(); } catch { /* */ }
  return { ok: true, detail: `${M2_TYPES.length} element types: mesh builders, palette schemas, quantities` };
}


===== FILE: src/modules/revit-gaps/m3-register.ts =====
/**
 * Milestone 3 registration: real plan content, true elevations and sections, PDF fills.
 *
 * Installs window.__obsViewExt, which three anchored lines (added by the installer) in
 * modules/views/generators/{plan,section,elevation}.ts call:
 *   planFilter(planView, def)  drop the base generator's geometry for elements that now get proper symbols
 *   plan(def, scene)           add poche, room tags, symbols, door marks
 *   section(def) / elevation(def)  replace the bounding-rectangle projections with real ones
 * and window.__obsM3FillPolygon, used by the PDF exporter (see m3-pdf-fill.ts).
 */
import { ctx } from "../../core/context";
import { notify } from "../../core/notify";
import { roomsStore } from "../rooms/rooms";
import { listViews, updateView } from "../views/store";
import { elementSolids, type SolidElement } from "./m3-solids";
import { cutSolids, capEntities, datumEntities, elevationFrame, entitiesBounds, projectSolids, sectionFrame, DEFAULT_STYLES } from "./m3-project";
import { DEFAULT_PLAN_OPTIONS, planExtras, type PlanExtras, type PlanOptions, type PlanRoom } from "./m3-plan";
import { fillPolygonPdf } from "./m3-pdf-fill";

type Any = Record<string, any>;
const authoring = (): Any | null => ((ctx.features as Any).authoring ?? null);
const KEY = "obs.m3.planOptions";

export function getPlanOptions(): PlanOptions {
  try { return { ...DEFAULT_PLAN_OPTIONS, ...JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "{}") }; } catch { return { ...DEFAULT_PLAN_OPTIONS }; }
}
export function setPlanOption<K extends keyof PlanOptions>(k: K, v: PlanOptions[K]): void {
  try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...getPlanOptions(), [k]: v })); } catch { /* */ }
}

function elements(): SolidElement[] { return (authoring()?.getElements?.() ?? []) as SolidElement[]; }
function levels(): Array<{ name: string; elevation: number }> {
  return ((authoring()?.getLevels?.() ?? []) as Any[]).map((l) => ({ name: String(l.name), elevation: Number(l.elevation) || 0 })).sort((a, b) => a.elevation - b.elevation);
}
function levelElevation(levelId: string | null): number {
  if (!levelId) return 0;
  return Number(((authoring()?.getLevels?.() ?? []) as Any[]).find((l) => l.id === levelId)?.elevation) || 0;
}

/** Same view range arithmetic as modules/drawings/plan-generator.ts. */
function planBand(def: Any): { cut: number; low: number; high: number } {
  const pl = def.plan;
  const lowDepth = Math.max(Math.abs(pl.bottomDepth ?? 0), Math.abs(pl.viewDepth ?? pl.bottomDepth ?? 0));
  const highDepth = Math.max(pl.cutHeight, Math.abs(pl.topHeight ?? pl.cutHeight));
  return { cut: pl.levelElevation + pl.cutHeight, low: pl.levelElevation - lowDepth, high: pl.levelElevation + highDepth };
}

function roomsFor(def: Any): PlanRoom[] {
  const elev = def.plan.levelElevation;
  return roomsStore.listRooms()
    .filter((r) => Math.abs(levelElevation(r.levelId) - elev) < 0.05)
    .map((r) => ({ id: r.id, name: r.name, number: r.number, area: r.area, levelId: r.levelId, boundary: r.boundary, labelPoint: r.properties?.labelPoint as [number, number] | undefined }));
}

export function computePlanExtras(def: Any): PlanExtras | null {
  if (!def?.plan) return null;
  const b = planBand(def);
  return planExtras({ elements: elements(), rooms: roomsFor(def), cutElevation: b.cut, bandLow: b.low, bandHigh: b.high, scale: def.scale || 100, kind: def.kind === "rcp" ? "rcp" : "plan", options: getPlanOptions() });
}

const POCHE: Record<string, string> = { wall: "#6b7280", slab: "#6b7280", roof: "#6b7280", column: "#6b7280", footing: "#6b7280" };
const pocheFor = (type: string) => POCHE[type] ?? "#9ca3af";
const LIGHT = { ...DEFAULT_STYLES, outline: { color: "#374151", widthPt: 0.18, dash: null }, crease: { color: "#6b7280", widthPt: 0.12, dash: null } };

function finishScene(scene: Any, extra: Any[]): void {
  const b = entitiesBounds([...scene.layers.project, ...scene.layers.cut, ...extra]);
  if (b) scene.bounds = b;
  scene.generatedAt = Date.now();
}

async function sectionScene(def: Any): Promise<Any | null> {
  if (!def.section) return null;
  const solids = elementSolids(elements());
  if (!solids.length) return null;
  const frame = sectionFrame(def.section.cutLine, def.section.lookDir === "left" ? "left" : "right");
  const { far, caps } = cutSolids(solids, frame);
  const scene: Any = { bounds: { minX: 0, minZ: 0, maxX: 10, maxZ: 10 }, layers: { cut: [], project: [], overhead: [], below: [], grid: [], annotation: [], symbols: [] }, generatedAt: Date.now() };
  scene.layers.project.push(...projectSolids(far, frame, { styles: LIGHT, farClip: def.section.farClip ?? 60 }));
  scene.layers.cut.push(...capEntities(caps, pocheFor));
  const b = entitiesBounds([...scene.layers.project, ...scene.layers.cut]);
  if (!b) return null;
  const lv = levels().filter((l) => l.elevation >= (def.section.bottom ?? -1e9) - 0.01 && l.elevation <= (def.section.top ?? 1e9) + 0.01);
  scene.layers.annotation.push(...datumEntities(lv, b.minX - 0.5, b.maxX + 0.5));
  finishScene(scene, scene.layers.annotation);
  return scene;
}

async function elevationScene(def: Any): Promise<Any | null> {
  if (!def.elevation) return null;
  const solids = elementSolids(elements());
  if (!solids.length) return null;
  const frame = elevationFrame(def.elevation.direction);
  const scene: Any = { bounds: { minX: 0, minZ: 0, maxX: 10, maxZ: 10 }, layers: { cut: [], project: [], overhead: [], below: [], grid: [], annotation: [], symbols: [] }, generatedAt: Date.now() };
  scene.layers.project.push(...projectSolids(solids, frame, { farClip: def.elevation.farClip ?? 100 }));
  const b = entitiesBounds(scene.layers.project);
  if (!b) return null;
  scene.layers.annotation.push(...datumEntities(levels().filter((l) => l.elevation >= b.minZ - 0.5 && l.elevation <= b.maxZ + 0.5), b.minX - 0.5, b.maxX + 0.5));
  finishScene(scene, scene.layers.annotation);
  return scene;
}

/** Force every saved view to regenerate (sheets cache scenes by view update time). */
export async function refreshViews(): Promise<void> {
  for (const v of listViews()) updateView(v.id, { updatedAt: Date.now() });
}

let installed = false;
export function setupM3(): { ok: boolean; detail: string } {
  if (installed) return { ok: true, detail: "already installed" };
  installed = true;
  const w = window as Any;
  w.__obsViewExt = {
    planFilter(planView: Any, def: Any) {
      const extras = computePlanExtras(def);
      def.__m3 = extras;
      if (!extras || !extras.suppressIds.size) return;
      planView.segments = planView.segments.filter((s: Any) => !s.source || !extras.suppressIds.has(s.source));
    },
    plan(def: Any, scene: Any) {
      const x: PlanExtras | null = def.__m3 ?? computePlanExtras(def);
      delete def.__m3;
      if (!x) return;
      scene.layers.cut.unshift(...x.under);
      scene.layers.cut.push(...x.cut);
      scene.layers.overhead.push(...x.overhead);
      scene.layers.symbols.push(...x.symbols);
      const b = entitiesBounds([...scene.layers.cut, ...scene.layers.overhead, ...scene.layers.symbols]);
      if (b) scene.bounds = { minX: Math.min(scene.bounds.minX, b.minX), minZ: Math.min(scene.bounds.minZ, b.minZ), maxX: Math.max(scene.bounds.maxX, b.maxX), maxZ: Math.max(scene.bounds.maxZ, b.maxZ) };
    },
    section: sectionScene,
    elevation: elevationScene,
  };
  w.__obsM3FillPolygon = fillPolygonPdf;
  w.__obsM3 = { getPlanOptions, setPlanOption, refreshViews };
  return { ok: true, detail: "plan content, true sections / elevations, PDF fills" };
}

export function togglePlanOption(k: keyof PlanOptions, label: string): void {
  const cur = getPlanOptions();
  const next = !cur[k];
  setPlanOption(k, next as never);
  void refreshViews();
  notify("info", `${label}: ${next ? "on" : "off"}`, { timeout: 1500 });
}


===== FILE: src/modules/standards/framework.ts =====
/**
 * Small helpers that make rule authors' lives easier.
 *
 * Every `prop*` helper returns undefined when the key is missing, so rules
 * can express "missing or wrong" without nesting null checks.
 */

/** Read a value from the flattened context props. */
export function getProp(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  key: string,
): unknown {
  if (!props) return undefined;
  if (key in props) return props[key];
  const lower = key.toLowerCase();
  for (const k of Object.keys(props)) {
    if (k.toLowerCase() === lower) return props[k];
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function propString(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  key: string,
): string | undefined {
  const v = getProp(props, key);
  if (v == null) return undefined;
  const s = String(v).trim();
  return s.length ? s : undefined;
}

export function propNumber(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  key: string,
): number | undefined {
  const v = getProp(props, key);
  if (v == null) return undefined;
  if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
  const s = String(v).trim();
  if (!s) return undefined;
  // Strip unit suffixes like "900", "900mm", "0.9 m".
  const m = s.match(/^(-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*(mm|cm|m|ft|in|Â°|deg)?$/i);
  if (!m) return undefined;
  let n = Number(m[1].replace(",", "."));
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return undefined;
  const unit = (m[2] ?? "").toLowerCase();
  // Convert everything to metres for length-like properties.
  if (unit === "mm") n /= 1000;
  else if (unit === "cm") n /= 100;
  else if (unit === "ft") n *= 0.3048;
  else if (unit === "in") n *= 0.0254;
  return n;
}

/** True if the property exists and is non-empty. */
export function hasProp(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  key: string,
): boolean {
  const v = getProp(props, key);
  return v != null && String(v).trim() !== "";
}

/** Return the first property that exists among a list of candidates. */
export function firstProp(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  keys: string[],
): unknown {
  for (const k of keys) {
    const v = getProp(props, k);
    if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== "") return v;
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function firstPropString(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  keys: string[],
): string | undefined {
  const v = firstProp(props, keys);
  if (v == null) return undefined;
  return String(v).trim() || undefined;
}

export function firstPropNumber(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  keys: string[],
): number | undefined {
  for (const k of keys) {
    const n = propNumber(props, k);
    if (n != null) return n;
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** Case-insensitive substring test on any property. */
export function anyPropContains(
  props: Record<string, any> | undefined,
  keys: string[],
  needle: string,
): boolean {
  const n = needle.toLowerCase();
  for (const k of keys) {
    const s = firstPropString(props, [k]);
    if (s && s.toLowerCase().includes(n)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Category matcher supporting a trailing '*' wildcard. */
export function categoryMatches(
  category: string,
  patterns: string[],
): boolean {
  const c = category.toUpperCase();
  for (const p of patterns) {
    const up = p.toUpperCase();
    if (up.endsWith("*")) {
      if (c.startsWith(up.slice(0, -1))) return true;
    } else if (c === up) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** Format a metre value for display in the "actual" / "required" columns. */
export function mm(meters: number): string {
  return `${Math.round(meters * 1000)} mm`;
}

export function m(meters: number): string {
  return `${meters.toFixed(2)} m`;
}

/** Package name and version helper for violations. */
export function packHeader(pack: {
  name: string;
  jurisdiction: string;
  version: string;
}): string {
  return `${pack.name} Â· ${pack.jurisdiction} Â· ${pack.version}`;
}

===== FILE: src/modules/standards/rules/engine.ts =====
/**
 * Standards rule engine.
 *
 * Runs one or more rule packs against a loaded fragment model. Handles:
 *   â€¢ Bounded concurrency (rules within a pack run sequentially; packs run
 *     one at a time to avoid starving the render loop)
 *   â€¢ Progress + cancellation
 *   â€¢ Deduplication across overlapping packs
 *   â€¢ Categorising violations by severity for the UI
 */
import { ctx } from "../../../core/context";
import { i18n } from "../../../core/i18n";
import { units } from "../../../core/units";
import { diagnostics } from "../../../core/diagnostics";
import { listPacks, getPack, listRules, getRule } from "./registry";
import type {
  Rule, RuleContext, RulePack, RuleViolation, RunOptions, RunSummary,
} from "./types";

export interface RunResult {
  summary: RunSummary;
  violations: RuleViolation[];
  errorsByRule: Map<string, string>;
}

export async function runRules(opts: RunOptions = {}): Promise<RunResult> {
  const start = Date.now();
  const signal = opts.signal;
  const progress = opts.onProgress ?? (() => {});

  const modelId =
    opts.modelId ??
    (ctx.fragments.list.size ? [...ctx.fragments.list.keys()][0] : null);
  if (!modelId) {
    return emptyResult(start, "No model loaded");
  }
  const model = ctx.fragments.list.get(modelId);
  if (!model) {
    return emptyResult(start, `Model "${modelId}" not loaded`);
  }

  // Determine the set of rules to run.
  let rules: Rule[] = [];
  if (opts.ruleIds?.length) {
    rules = opts.ruleIds.map((id) => getRule(id)).filter((r): r is Rule => !!r);
  } else if (opts.packIds?.length) {
    for (const pid of opts.packIds) {
      const p = getPack(pid);
      if (p) rules.push(...p.rules);
    }
  } else {
    rules = listRules();
  }

  // De-duplicate by rule id (a rule may be shared by multiple packs).
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  rules = rules.filter((r) => {
    if (seen.has(r.id)) return false;
    seen.add(r.id);
    return true;
  });

  if (!rules.length) {
    return emptyResult(start, "No rules selected");
  }

  // Model category catalogue for early exit filtering.
  let allCats: string[] = [];
  try {
    const cats = await model.getItemsOfCategories([/.+/]);
    allCats = Object.keys(cats);
  } catch { /* */ }

  const violations: RuleViolation[] = [];
  const errorsByRule = new Map<string, string>();
  const ruleCtx: RuleContext = {
    model,
    modelId,
    locale: i18n.getLocale(),
    unitSystem: units.getSystem(),
    progress: (done: number, total: number, note?: string) => progress(done, total, note),
    isCancelled: () => signal?.cancelled === true,
  };

  let ruleIndex = 0;
  for (const rule of rules) {
    if (signal?.cancelled) break;
    ruleIndex++;
    progress(ruleIndex, rules.length, rule.name);

    if (rule.requiresCategories && !rule.requiresCategories(allCats)) {
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const found = await rule.check(ruleCtx);
      if (Array.isArray(found)) {
        if (found.length) violations.push(...found);
      } else if (found.violations?.length) {
        violations.push(...found.violations);
      } else if (!found.pass) {
        violations.push({
          ruleId: rule.id,
          ruleName: rule.name,
          standard: rule.standard ?? "Standard",
          jurisdiction: rule.jurisdiction ?? "intl",
          severity: rule.severity,
          category: rule.category ?? "General",
          elementId: 0,
          elementName: "",
          elementCategory: "",
          message: found.message ?? "Rule violation",
          actual: found.actual,
          required: found.required,
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      errorsByRule.set(rule.id, msg);
      diagnostics.warn("standards.rules", `rule "${rule.id}" threw: ${msg}`, err);
    }

    // Yield to the render loop between rules.
    await new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }

  const finishedAt = Date.now();
  let errors = 0, warnings = 0, infos = 0;
  for (const v of violations) {
    if (v.severity === "error") errors++;
    else if (v.severity === "warning") warnings++;
    else infos++;
  }

  const summary: RunSummary = {
    packCount: opts.packIds?.length ?? listPacks().length,
    ruleCount: rules.length,
    violations: violations.length,
    errors, warnings, infos,
    startedAt: start,
    finishedAt,
    durationMs: finishedAt - start,
    cancelled: signal?.cancelled === true,
  };

  console.log(
    `[standards] ran ${rules.length} rule(s) in ${summary.durationMs} ms Â· ` +
    `${violations.length} violation(s) Â· ${errorsByRule.size} rule error(s)`,
  );

  return { summary, violations, errorsByRule };
}

function emptyResult(start: number, reason: string): RunResult {
  console.warn("[standards]", reason);
  const now = Date.now();
  return {
    summary: {
      packCount: 0, ruleCount: 0, violations: 0,
      errors: 0, warnings: 0, infos: 0,
      startedAt: start, finishedAt: now, durationMs: now - start, cancelled: false,
    },
    violations: [],
    errorsByRule: new Map([[reason, reason]]),
  };
}

===== FILE: src/modules/schedules/schedule-generator.ts =====
/**
 * Schedule generator v2.
 *
 * New in this revision:
 *   â€¢ Formula columns (evaluated per row via schedule-formula.ts)
 *   â€¢ Live quantities â€” Area/Volume/Length pulled from getElementTriangles
 *   â€¢ Live cost â€” Cost item, unit rate, and line cost resolved per row
 *   â€¢ Multi-level grouping with subtotals
 *   â€¢ Conditional formatting rule application (returns hints to the panel)
 *   â€¢ Aggregate formulas via [SUM], [AVG], [COUNT] over group scope
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../../core/context";
import { getElements } from "../../features/authoring";
import { getElementTriangles } from "../clash/geometry";
import { computeTakeoff, getRows as getTakeoffRows } from "../cost/quantities";
import { computeLine } from "../cost/cost";
import type { AuthoredElement } from "../projects/types";
import type {
  ScheduleColumn,
  ScheduleDefinition,
  ScheduleRow,
  ScheduleRun,
  GroupLevel,
} from "./schedule-types";
import { compileFormula, validateFormula } from "./schedule-formula";

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Geometry math (shared with quantities.ts)
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function signedVolume(tris: Float32Array[]): number {
  let v = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    const ax = t[0], ay = t[1], az = t[2];
    const bx = t[3], by = t[4], bz = t[5];
    const cx = t[6], cy = t[7], cz = t[8];
    v += (ax * (by * cz - bz * cy) + ay * (bz * cx - bx * cz) + az * (bx * cy - by * cx)) / 6;
  }
  return Math.abs(v);
}

function surfaceArea(tris: Float32Array[]): number {
  let s = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    const ux = t[3] - t[0], uy = t[4] - t[1], uz = t[5] - t[2];
    const vx = t[6] - t[0], vy = t[7] - t[1], vz = t[8] - t[2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy;
    const ny = uz * vx - ux * vz;
    const nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    s += 0.5 * Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz);
  }
  return s;
}

function bboxSize(b: THREE.Box3): THREE.Vector3 {
  const v = new THREE.Vector3();
  b.getSize(v);
  return v;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Helpers
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function shortCat(cat: string): string {
  return cat.replace(/^IFC/i, "").replace(/STANDARDCASE$/i, "") || cat;
}

function r3(n: number): number {
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000 : 0;
}

function levelNameForElevation(elevation: number): string {
  return `Level ${Math.max(0, Math.round(elevation / 3))}`;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Takeoff integration
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
let takeoffCache: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof computeTakeoff>> | null = null;
let takeoffCacheAt = 0;

async function ensureTakeoff(): Promise<void> {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (takeoffCache && now - takeoffCacheAt < 30_000) return;
  takeoffCache = await computeTakeoff();
  takeoffCacheAt = now;
}

function lookupTakeoff(key: string) {
  if (!takeoffCache) return null;
  return takeoffCache.find((r) => r.key === key) ?? null;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Authored rows
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function matchesCategory(el: AuthoredElement, category: string | null): boolean {
  if (!category) return true;
  const c = category.toUpperCase();
  const t = el.type.toUpperCase();
  if (c === "IFCDOOR") return t === "DOOR";
  if (c === "IFCWINDOW") return t === "WINDOW";
  if (c === "IFCWALL") return t === "WALL";
  if (c === "IFCSLAB") return t === "SLAB";
  if (c === "IFCCOLUMN") return t === "COLUMN";
  if (c === "IFCBEAM") return t === "BEAM";
  if (c === "IFCMEMBER") return t === "BRACE" || t === "TRUSS";
  if (c === "IFCFOOTING") return t === "FOOTING" || t === "STRIP";
  if (c === "IFCDUCTSEGMENT") return t === "DUCT";
  if (c === "IFCPIPESEGMENT") return t === "PIPE";
  if (c === "IFCCABLECARRIERSEGMENT") return t === "CABLE-TRAY";
  if (c === "IFCCONDUITSEGMENT") return t === "CONDUIT";
  return true;
}

function authoredRow(el: AuthoredElement, def: ScheduleDefinition): ScheduleRow {
  const p = el.params;
  const values: Record<string, string | number | null> = {
    mark: el.name,
    name: el.name,
    type: p.doorPanel || p.windowPanel ? `${el.type} (${p.doorPanel ?? p.windowPanel})` : el.type,
    level: levelNameForElevation(p.elevation),
    category: shortCat(el.type.toUpperCase()),
    width: r3(
      p.width ??
        (p.start && p.end
          ? Math.hypot(p.end[0] - p.start[0], p.end[1] - p.start[1])
          : 0),
    ),
    height: r3(p.height ?? 0),
    depth: r3(p.depth ?? 0),
    thickness: r3(p.thickness),
    elevation: r3(p.elevation),
    material: String((el.properties as any)?.material ?? ""),
    fireRating: String((el.properties as any)?.fireRating ?? ""),
    panel: p.doorPanel ?? p.windowPanel ?? null,
    glazing: p.windowPanel ?? null,
    systemType: String((el.properties as any)?.systemType ?? ""),
    count: 1,
  };

  // Live quantities â€” pull from the takeoff engine when enabled.
  if (def.useLiveQuantities && takeoffCache) {
    const t = lookupTakeoff(`authored:${el.id}`);
    if (t) {
      values.length = t.length;
      values.area = t.area;
      values.volume = t.volume;
    }
  }

  return {
    id: `authored:${el.id}`,
    source: "authored",
    values,
    link: { kind: "authored", authoredId: el.id },
  };
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   IFC rows
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
interface IfcCacheEntry {
  ids: number[];
  categoryBy: Map<number, string>;
  meta: Map<number, Record<string, string>>;
  boxes: THREE.Box3[];
  matrix: THREE.Matrix4;
  name: string;
}
const ifcCache = new Map<string, IfcCacheEntry>();

async function buildIfcCache(modelId: string, model: any): Promise<void> {
  if (ifcCache.has(modelId)) return;

  let ids: number[] = [];
  let boxes: THREE.Box3[] = [];
  try {
    ids = ((await (model as any).getItemsIdsWithGeometry?.()) as number[]) ?? [];
    boxes = await (model as any).getBoxes(ids);
  } catch {
    ifcCache.set(modelId, {
      ids: [],
      categoryBy: new Map(),
      meta: new Map(),
      boxes: [],
      matrix: new THREE.Matrix4(),
      name: modelId,
    });
    return;
  }

  const categoryBy = new Map<number, string>();
  try {
    const cats = await (model as any).getItemsOfCategories([/.+/]);
    for (const [c, list] of Object.entries(cats ?? {})) {
      for (const id of (list as number[]) ?? []) categoryBy.set(id, c);
    }
  } catch {
    /* */
  }

  // Attributes â€” batch in chunks of 500.
  const meta = new Map<number, Record<string, string>>();
  const CHUNK = 500;
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += CHUNK) {
    const slice = ids.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
    try {
      const data = await (model as any).getItemsData(slice, {
        attributesDefault: false,
        attributes: ["Name", "ObjectType", "Tag", "Description", "PredefinedType", "FireRating"],
      });
      for (let j = 0; j < slice.length; j++) {
        const d = (data as any)?.[j] ?? {};
        const m: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (const k of Object.keys(d)) {
          const v = d[k]?.value ?? d[k];
          if (v != null && typeof v !== "object") m[k] = String(v);
        }
        meta.set(slice[j], m);
      }
    } catch {
      /* */
    }
  }

  try {
    model.object?.updateMatrixWorld?.(true);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  const matrix =
    (model.object?.matrixWorld?.clone?.() as THREE.Matrix4) ?? new THREE.Matrix4();

  ifcCache.set(modelId, {
    ids,
    categoryBy,
    meta,
    boxes,
    matrix,
    name: (model as any)?.name ?? modelId,
  });
}

export function clearIfcScheduleCache(): void {
  ifcCache.clear();
  takeoffCache = null;
  takeoffCacheAt = 0;
}

function matchesIfcCategory(rawCat: string, wanted: string | null): boolean {
  if (!wanted) return true;
  const w = wanted.toUpperCase();
  const c = rawCat.toUpperCase();
  return c === w || c.startsWith(w);
}

async function ifcRows(def: ScheduleDefinition, signal?: { cancelled: boolean }): Promise<ScheduleRow[]> {
  await ensureTakeoff();
  const out: ScheduleRow[] = [];

  for (const [modelId, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
    if (signal?.cancelled) break;
    await buildIfcCache(modelId, model);
    const cache = ifcCache.get(modelId);
    if (!cache || !cache.ids.length) continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < cache.ids.length; i++) {
      if (signal?.cancelled) break;
      const id = cache.ids[i];
      const rawCat = cache.categoryBy.get(id) ?? "Other";
      if (!matchesIfcCategory(rawCat, def.category)) continue;

      const b = cache.boxes[i];
      if (!b || b.isEmpty()) continue;
      const world = b.clone().applyMatrix4(cache.matrix);
      const size = bboxSize(world);
      const meta = cache.meta.get(id) ?? {};
      const name = meta.Name ?? meta.ObjectType ?? meta.Tag ?? `#${id}`;

      const values: Record<string, string | number | null> = {
        mark: name,
        name,
        type: meta.ObjectType ?? shortCat(rawCat),
        level: null,
        category: shortCat(rawCat),
        width: r3(Math.max(size.x, size.z)),
        height: r3(size.y),
        depth: r3(Math.min(size.x, size.z)),
        thickness: r3(Math.min(size.x, size.z)),
        area: r3(2 * (size.x * size.y + size.x * size.z + size.y * size.z)),
        volume: r3(Math.abs(size.x * size.y * size.z)),
        fireRating: meta.FireRating ?? "",
        material: meta.Material ?? "",
        count: 1,
      };

      // Live quantities â€” prefer geometric extraction when triangles exist.
      if (def.useLiveQuantities) {
        try {
          const tris = getElementTriangles(model.object, id);
          if (tris && tris.length) {
            values.volume = r3(signedVolume(tris));
            values.area = r3(surfaceArea(tris));
            const diag = Math.hypot(size.x, size.y, size.z);
            values.length = r3(diag * 0.9);
          }
        } catch {
          /* fall back to bbox */
        }
      }

      out.push({
        id: `ifc:${modelId}:${id}`,
        source: "ifc",
        values,
        link: { kind: "ifc", modelId, localId: id },
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Cost attachment
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function attachCost(row: ScheduleRow): void {
  try {
    const line = computeLine({
      key: row.id,
      source: row.source === "ifc" ? "ifc" : "authored",
      label: String(row.values.name ?? ""),
      detail: "",
      count: Number(row.values.count ?? 1),
      length: Number(row.values.length ?? 0),
      area: Number(row.values.area ?? 0),
      volume: Number(row.values.volume ?? 0),
    } as any);
    if (line.item) {
      row.values.costItem = `${line.item.code} Â· ${line.item.name}`;
      row.values.unitRate = line.rate;
      row.values.lineCost = r3(line.lineCost);
    } else {
      row.values.costItem = "";
      row.values.unitRate = 0;
      row.values.lineCost = 0;
    }
  } catch {
    row.values.costItem = "";
    row.values.unitRate = 0;
    row.values.lineCost = 0;
  }
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Formulas
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
type CompiledCol = { key: string; fn: ReturnType<typeof compileFormula> };

function compileFormulaColumns(def: ScheduleDefinition): CompiledCol[] {
  const out: CompiledCol[] = [];
  for (const col of def.columns) {
    if (col.format !== "formula") continue;
    if (!col.formula) continue;
    const v = validateFormula(col.formula);
    if (!v.ok) {
      console.warn(`[schedule] invalid formula for "${col.key}":`, v.error);
      continue;
    }
    out.push({ key: col.key, fn: compileFormula(col.formula) });
  }
  return out;
}

/** Evaluate every formula column for every row, using group-scope aggregates. */
function evaluateFormulas(rows: ScheduleRow[], compiled: CompiledCol[]): void {
  if (!compiled.length || !rows.length) return;

  // Precompute aggregate accessors per group for each formula column.
  // We compute them on the fly per row (small dataset).
  for (const row of rows) {
    for (const { key, fn } of compiled) {
      const ctxAgg = {
        sum: (header: string) => aggregateOver(rows, header, row, "sum"),
        avg: (header: string) => aggregateOver(rows, header, row, "avg"),
        count: (header: string) => aggregateOver(rows, header, row, "count"),
        min: (header: string) => aggregateOver(rows, header, row, "min"),
        max: (header: string) => aggregateOver(rows, header, row, "max"),
      };
      try {
        const v = fn({ values: row.values, aggregates: ctxAgg });
        row.values[key] = Number.isFinite(v) ? r3(v) : 0;
      } catch (err) {
        row.values[key] = 0;
      }
    }
  }
}

function aggregateOver(
  rows: ScheduleRow[],
  header: string,
  scopeRow: ScheduleRow,
  op: "sum" | "avg" | "count" | "min" | "max",
): number {
  // For now, aggregate over all rows of the same source-kind.
  // Group-scoped aggregation is a later enhancement.
  const sameKind = rows.filter((r) => !r.subtotal && r.source === scopeRow.source);
  const vals: number[] = [];
  for (const r of sameKind) {
    const v = headerValue(r, header);
    const n = typeof v === "number" ? v : parseFloat(String(v ?? ""));
    if (Number.isFinite(n)) vals.push(n);
  }
  if (!vals.length) return 0;
  switch (op) {
    case "sum": return vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    case "avg": return vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length;
    case "count": return vals.length;
    case "min": return Math.min(...vals);
    case "max": return Math.max(...vals);
  }
}

function headerValue(row: ScheduleRow, header: string): string | number | null {
  if (header in row.values) return row.values[header];
  const lower = header.toLowerCase();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(row.values)) {
    if (k.toLowerCase() === lower) return v;
  }
  return null;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Grouping
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function bucketValue(value: any, bucket: GroupLevel["bucket"]): string | number {
  if (bucket === "thousands") {
    const n = parseFloat(String(value ?? ""));
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.floor(n / 1000) * 1000 : "â€”";
  }
  if (bucket === "firstLetter") {
    const s = String(value ?? "");
    return s ? s[0].toUpperCase() : "â€”";
  }
  if (bucket === "ifcPrefix") {
    const s = String(value ?? "").replace(/^IFC/i, "");
    return s.slice(0, 4).toUpperCase() || "â€”";
  }
  if (bucket === "level") {
    return String(value ?? "â€”") || "â€”";
  }
  return String(value ?? "â€”") || "â€”";
}

/** Attach `groupPath` to each row based on the definition's group levels. */
function assignGroupPath(rows: ScheduleRow[], levels: GroupLevel[]): void {
  if (!levels.length) return;
  for (const row of rows) {
    const path: (string | number)[] = [];
    for (const lvl of levels) {
      const v = headerValue(row, lvl.key);
      path.push(bucketValue(v, lvl.bucket));
    }
    row.groupPath = path;
  }
}

/** Build subtotal rows per group level. Returns a new array with rows
 *  reordered by group and subtotal rows inserted. */
function buildGroupedRows(
  rows: ScheduleRow[],
  levels: GroupLevel[],
  numericColumns: string[],
): ScheduleRow[] {
  if (!levels.length) return rows;

  const out: ScheduleRow[] = [];
  const recursion = (subset: ScheduleRow[], level: number, prefix: (string | number)[]) => {
    if (level >= levels.length) {
      for (const r of subset) out.push(r);
      return;
    }
    const buckets = new Map<string | number, ScheduleRow[]>();
    for (const r of subset) {
      const key = r.groupPath?.[level] ?? "â€”";
      const arr = buckets.get(key) ?? [];
      arr.push(r);
      buckets.set(key, arr);
    }
    const sortedKeys = [...buckets.keys()].sort((a, b) =>
      String(a).localeCompare(String(b)),
    );

    for (const k of sortedKeys) {
      const group = buckets.get(k)!;
      // Emit a header row at this level.
      const headerRow: ScheduleRow = {
        id: `group:${prefix.concat(k).join("|")}`,
        source: "mixed",
        values: {},
        groupPath: prefix.concat(k),
        depth: level,
        subtotal: true,
      };
      const firstColKey = numericColumns[0] ? undefined : undefined;
      const labelCol = levels[level].key;
      headerRow.values[labelCol] = k;
      out.push(headerRow);

      // Recurse or emit data rows
      recursion(group, level + 1, prefix.concat(k));

      // Emit subtotal after this group if it's the last level or subtotal is on.
      const subtotalEnabled = levels[level].subtotal !== false;
      if (subtotalEnabled) {
        const subtotalRow: ScheduleRow = {
          id: `subtotal:${prefix.concat(k).join("|")}`,
          source: "mixed",
          values: {},
          groupPath: prefix.concat(k),
          depth: level,
          subtotal: true,
          subtotalValues: {},
        };
        for (const colKey of numericColumns) {
          let s = 0;
          for (const r of group) {
            const v = headerValue(r, colKey);
            const n = typeof v === "number" ? v : parseFloat(String(v ?? ""));
            if (Number.isFinite(n)) s += n;
          }
          subtotalRow.subtotalValues![colKey] = r3(s);
        }
        subtotalRow.values[levels[level].key] = `Subtotal Â· ${k}`;
        out.push(subtotalRow);
      }
    }
  };
  recursion(rows, 0, []);
  return out;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Public
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
export interface RunScheduleOptions {
  signal?: { cancelled: boolean };
  onProgress?: (p: { phase: string; current: number; total: number }) => void;
}

export async function runSchedule(
  def: ScheduleDefinition,
  opts: RunScheduleOptions = {},
): Promise<ScheduleRun> {
  const onProg = opts.onProgress ?? (() => {});

  onProg({ phase: "Reading authored elements", current: 0, total: 3 });

  const authoredRows: ScheduleRow[] = [];
  for (const el of getElements()) {
    if (opts.signal?.cancelled) break;
    if (!matchesCategory(el, def.category)) continue;
    if (def.levelName && levelNameForElevation(el.params.elevation) !== def.levelName) continue;
    const row = authoredRow(el, def);
    if (def.useCostData) attachCost(row);
    authoredRows.push(row);
  }

  onProg({ phase: "Reading IFC elements", current: 1, total: 3 });
  const ifc = await ifcRows(def, opts.signal);
  if (def.useCostData) for (const r of ifc) attachCost(r);

  onProg({ phase: "Grouping and totals", current: 2, total: 3 });
  let all: ScheduleRow[] = [...authoredRows, ...ifc];

  // Sorting
  const sortKeys = def.sortKeys ?? (def.sortBy ? [{ key: def.sortBy, dir: "asc" } as const] : []);
  if (sortKeys.length) {
    all.sort((a, b) => {
      for (const sk of sortKeys) {
        const va = headerValue(a, sk.key);
        const vb = headerValue(b, sk.key);
        if (va == null && vb == null) continue;
        if (va == null) return sk.dir === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
        if (vb == null) return sk.dir === "asc" ? -1 : 1;
        if (typeof va === "number" && typeof vb === "number") {
          if (va !== vb) return (va - vb) * (sk.dir === "asc" ? 1 : -1);
        } else {
          const cmp = String(va).localeCompare(String(vb));
          if (cmp !== 0) return cmp * (sk.dir === "asc" ? 1 : -1);
        }
      }
      return 0;
    });
  }

  // Formula columns
  const compiled = compileFormulaColumns(def);
  if (compiled.length) evaluateFormulas(all, compiled);

  // Grand totals (before grouping so we don't count subtotal rows)
  const numericCols = def.columns
    .filter((c) => c.format !== "text" && c.format !== "date")
    .map((c) => c.key);
  const totals: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const c of numericCols) {
    let s = 0;
    for (const r of all) {
      const v = r.values[c];
      const n = typeof v === "number" ? v : parseFloat(String(v ?? ""));
      if (Number.isFinite(n)) s += n;
    }
    totals[c] = r3(s);
  }

  // Grouping
  if (def.grouping?.length) {
    assignGroupPath(all, def.grouping);
    all = buildGroupedRows(all, def.grouping, numericCols);
  }

  onProg({ phase: "Done", current: 3, total: 3 });

  return {
    definition: def,
    rows: all,
    totals,
    generatedAt: Date.now(),
  };
}

export async function runBuiltInSchedule(
  id: string,
  opts?: RunScheduleOptions,
): Promise<ScheduleRun | null> {
  const { getTemplate } = await import("./schedule-templates");
  const def = getTemplate(id);
  if (!def) return null;
  return runSchedule(def, opts);
}

===== FILE: src/modules/cost/quantities.ts =====
/**
 * Takeoff engine v3.1.
 *
 * Authored elements: exact quantities from parametric definitions.
 *   - Doors, windows, and fittings are billed by the piece (count = 1).
 *   - Linear elements (walls, beams, pipes, ducts) report length + area + volume.
 *   - Slabs and footings report area + volume.
 *
 * IFC elements: reads Qto_*BaseQuantities psets when present (author-provided
 * numbers) and computes geometric values in parallel from triangle soups.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../../core/context";
import { getElements } from "../../features/authoring";
import { getElementTriangles, type Tri } from "../clash/geometry";
import type { TakeoffRow } from "./types";

let rows: TakeoffRow[] = [];
export function getRows(): TakeoffRow[] {
  return rows;
}

export function shortCat(cat: string) {
  return cat.replace(/^IFC/i, "").replace(/STANDARDCASE$/i, "") || cat;
}

function r3(n: number) {
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000 : 0;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Geometry math
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

function signedVolume(tris: Tri[]): number {
  let v = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    const ax = t[0], ay = t[1], az = t[2];
    const bx = t[3], by = t[4], bz = t[5];
    const cx = t[6], cy = t[7], cz = t[8];
    const crX = by * cz - bz * cy;
    const crY = bz * cx - bx * cz;
    const crZ = bx * cy - by * cx;
    v += (ax * crX + ay * crY + az * crZ) / 6;
  }
  return Math.abs(v);
}

function surfaceArea(tris: Tri[]): number {
  let s = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    const ux = t[3] - t[0];
    const uy = t[4] - t[1];
    const uz = t[5] - t[2];
    const vx = t[6] - t[0];
    const vy = t[7] - t[1];
    const vz = t[8] - t[2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy;
    const ny = uz * vx - ux * vz;
    const nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    s += 0.5 * Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz);
  }
  return s;
}

function principalLength(tris: Tri[], iterations = 24): number {
  let n = 0;
  let cx = 0, cy = 0, cz = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    cx += t[0] + t[3] + t[6];
    cy += t[1] + t[4] + t[7];
    cz += t[2] + t[5] + t[8];
    n += 3;
  }
  if (!n) return 0;
  cx /= n; cy /= n; cz /= n;

  let xx = 0, xy = 0, xz = 0, yy = 0, yz = 0, zz = 0;
  for (const t of tris) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const dx = t[k * 3] - cx;
      const dy = t[k * 3 + 1] - cy;
      const dz = t[k * 3 + 2] - cz;
      xx += dx * dx; xy += dx * dy; xz += dx * dz;
      yy += dy * dy; yz += dy * dz; zz += dz * dz;
    }
  }

  let vx = 1, vy = 1, vz = 1;
  for (let it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
    const nx = xx * vx + xy * vy + xz * vz;
    const ny = xy * vx + yy * vy + yz * vz;
    const nz = xz * vx + yz * vy + zz * vz;
    const len = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1;
    vx = nx / len; vy = ny / len; vz = nz / len;
  }

  let min = Infinity, max = -Infinity;
  for (const t of tris) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const p = t[k * 3] * vx + t[k * 3 + 1] * vy + t[k * 3 + 2] * vz;
      if (p < min) min = p;
      if (p > max) max = p;
    }
  }
  return Math.max(0, max - min);
}

function trustedVolume(tris: Tri[], boxVolume: number): number {
  const v = signedVolume(tris);
  if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) return boxVolume;
  if (boxVolume > 0 && v > boxVolume * 1.05) return boxVolume;
  if (boxVolume > 0 && v < boxVolume * 0.02) return Math.min(v, boxVolume * 0.95);
  return v;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Qto extraction from IFC psets
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

interface QtoData {
  length: number;
  area: number;
  volume: number;
  found: boolean;
  sourceSet?: string;
}

function num(v: any): number | null {
  if (v == null) return null;
  const inner = typeof v === "object" && "value" in v ? (v as any).value : v;
  if (typeof inner === "number" && Number.isFinite(inner)) return inner;
  if (typeof inner === "string") {
    const n = parseFloat(inner);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
  }
  return null;
}

const AREA_KEYS = [
  "NetSideArea", "GrossSideArea", "NetArea", "GrossArea",
  "NetFloorArea", "GrossFloorArea",
  "NetFootprintArea", "GrossFootprintArea",
  "OuterSurfaceArea", "CrossSectionArea", "Area",
];
const VOLUME_KEYS = ["NetVolume", "GrossVolume", "Volume"];
const LENGTH_KEYS = ["Length", "Width", "Span"];

function pick(props: Record<string, any>, keys: string[]): number | null {
  for (const k of keys) {
    const v = num(props[k]);
    if (v != null) return v;
  }
  return null;
}

function extractQtoFromPsets(
  psets: Record<string, Record<string, any>>,
): QtoData | null {
  let length = 0, area = 0, volume = 0, found = false;
  let sourceSet: string | undefined;

  for (const [psName, props] of Object.entries(psets)) {
    if (!/^Qto_/i.test(psName)) continue;
    if (!props || typeof props !== "object") continue;

    const L = pick(props, LENGTH_KEYS);
    const A = pick(props, AREA_KEYS);
    const V = pick(props, VOLUME_KEYS);

    if (L != null) { length += L; found = true; sourceSet = psName; }
    if (A != null) { area += A; found = true; sourceSet = psName; }
    if (V != null) { volume += V; found = true; sourceSet = psName; }
  }

  return found ? { length, area, volume, found, sourceSet } : null;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Authored elements (exact, parametric)
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

function shoelaceArea(poly: [number, number][]): number {
  let s = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) {
    const [x1, z1] = poly[i];
    const [x2, z2] = poly[(i + 1) % poly.length];
    s += x1 * z2 - x2 * z1;
  }
  return Math.abs(s) / 2;
}

function authoredRows(): TakeoffRow[] {
  const out: TakeoffRow[] = [];
  for (const el of getElements()) {
    const p = el.params;
    let length = 0, area = 0, volume = 0;
    let detailSuffix = "exact";

    if ((el.type === "wall" || el.type === "strip") && p.start && p.end) {
      const L = Math.hypot(p.end[0] - p.start[0], p.end[1] - p.start[1]);
      length = L;
      area = L * p.height * 2;
      volume = L * p.height * p.thickness;
    } else if (el.type === "slab" && p.polygon && p.polygon.length >= 3) {
      const A = shoelaceArea(p.polygon);
      area = A;
      volume = A * p.height;
    } else if (el.type === "column" && p.point) {
      const w = p.thickness;
      const d = p.depth ?? p.thickness;
      length = p.height;
      area = 2 * (w + d) * p.height;
      volume = w * d * p.height;
    } else if (el.type === "beam" && p.start && p.end) {
      const L = Math.hypot(p.end[0] - p.start[0], p.end[1] - p.start[1]);
      length = L;
      volume = L * p.height * p.thickness;
      area = 2 * L * (p.height + p.thickness);
    } else if (el.type === "brace" && p.start && p.end) {
      const L = Math.hypot(
        p.end[0] - p.start[0],
        p.end[1] - p.start[1],
        p.height,
      );
      length = L;
      volume = L * p.thickness * p.thickness;
    } else if (el.type === "truss" && p.start && p.end) {
      const L = Math.hypot(p.end[0] - p.start[0], p.end[1] - p.start[1]);
      const t = p.thickness;
      const panels = Math.max(2, Number(el.properties?.panels ?? 4));
      const topChord = 2 * Math.hypot(L / 2, p.height);
      const posts = (panels - 1) * p.height * 0.6;
      const totalLen = L + topChord + posts;
      length = totalLen;
      volume = totalLen * t * t;
    } else if (el.type === "footing" && p.point) {
      const w = p.thickness;
      const d = p.depth ?? p.thickness;
      volume = w * d * p.height;
      area = 2 * (w * d) + 2 * (w + d) * p.height;
    } else if (el.type === "mline") {
      length =
        p.start && p.end
          ? Math.hypot(p.end[0] - p.start[0], p.end[1] - p.start[1])
          : 0;
    } else if (el.type === "door" || el.type === "window") {
      // Doors and windows are counted by the piece.
      detailSuffix = `unit (${p.width ?? 0.9} Ã— ${p.height} m)`;
    } else if (el.type === "fitting") {
      // Fittings are counted by the piece â€” they inherit their parent's
      // profile but the profile itself is not a separately billable
      // length / area / volume in a QS takeoff.
      const parent = (p.parentType as string) || "run";
      const profileLabel =
        parent === "pipe" || parent === "conduit"
          ? `Ã˜ ${(p.thickness * 1000).toFixed(0)} mm`
          : `${(p.thickness * 1000).toFixed(0)} Ã— ${(p.height * 1000).toFixed(0)} mm`;
      detailSuffix = `${parent} fitting (${profileLabel})`;
    }

    out.push({
      key: `authored:${el.id}`,
      source: "authored",
      label: el.name,
      detail: `${el.type} (authored, ${detailSuffix})`,
      count: 1,
      length: r3(length),
      area: r3(area),
      volume: r3(volume),
      primarySource: "authored",
    });
  }
  return out;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// IFC aggregation
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

const LINEAR_CAT =
  /^(WALL|BEAM|COLUMN|MEMBER|PILE|PIPESEGMENT|PIPEFITTING|DUCTSEGMENT|DUCTFITTING|CABLECARRIERSEGMENT|CABLETRAY|CONDUIT|RAILING|CURTAINWALL|PLATE)/i;

interface Agg {
  count: number;
  qto: { length: number; area: number; volume: number; elements: number };
  geom: { length: number; area: number; volume: number; elements: number };
  bboxOnly: number;
}

async function readQtoBatches(
  model: any,
  ids: number[],
): Promise<Map<number, QtoData>> {
  const out = new Map<number, QtoData>();
  const CHUNK = 500;
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += CHUNK) {
    const batch = ids.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
    try {
      const data = await model.getItemsData(batch, {
        attributesDefault: false,
        psetsDefault: true,
      } as any);
      for (let j = 0; j < batch.length; j++) {
        const d = (data as any[])?.[j];
        const psets = d?.psets;
        if (!psets || typeof psets !== "object") continue;
        const q = extractQtoFromPsets(psets);
        if (q) out.set(batch[j], q);
      }
    } catch {
      /* */
    }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }
  return out;
}

async function ifcRows(): Promise<TakeoffRow[]> {
  const out: TakeoffRow[] = [];

  for (const [modelId, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
    try {
      const ids: number[] = await model.getItemsIdsWithGeometry();
      if (!ids?.length) continue;

      const boxes = await model.getBoxes(ids);
      let cats: Record<string, number[]> = {};
      try {
        cats = await model.getItemsOfCategories([/.+/]);
      } catch { /* */ }
      const idToCat = new Map<string, string>();
      for (const [cat, list] of Object.entries(cats)) {
        for (const id of (list as number[]) || []) idToCat.set(String(id), cat);
      }

      const qtoMap = await readQtoBatches(model, ids);

      const agg = new Map<string, Agg>();
      const s = new THREE.Vector3();

      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        const b = boxes[i];
        if (!b || b.isEmpty()) continue;

        const localId = ids[i];
        const rawCat = idToCat.get(String(localId)) ?? "Other";
        const shortName = shortCat(rawCat);
        const key = `ifc:${modelId}:${shortName}`;

        let a = agg.get(key);
        if (!a) {
          a = {
            count: 0,
            qto: { length: 0, area: 0, volume: 0, elements: 0 },
            geom: { length: 0, area: 0, volume: 0, elements: 0 },
            bboxOnly: 0,
          };
          agg.set(key, a);
        }
        a.count++;

        const q = qtoMap.get(localId);
        if (q?.found) {
          a.qto.elements++;
          a.qto.length += q.length;
          a.qto.area += q.area;
          a.qto.volume += q.volume;
        }

        b.getSize(s);
        const boxVol = Math.abs(s.x * s.y * s.z);
        let tris: Tri[] | null = null;
        try {
          tris = getElementTriangles(model.object, localId);
        } catch {
          tris = null;
        }

        if (tris && tris.length) {
          a.geom.elements++;
          a.geom.volume += trustedVolume(tris, boxVol);
          a.geom.area += surfaceArea(tris);
          if (LINEAR_CAT.test(shortName)) {
            a.geom.length += principalLength(tris);
          }
        } else {
          a.bboxOnly++;
          a.geom.elements++;
          a.geom.volume += boxVol;
          a.geom.area += 2 * (s.x * s.y + s.x * s.z + s.y * s.z);
          if (LINEAR_CAT.test(shortName)) {
            a.geom.length += Math.max(s.x, s.y, s.z);
          }
        }
      }

      for (const [key, a] of agg) {
        const parts = key.split(":");
        const cat = parts.slice(2).join(":");

        const qtoShare = a.count > 0 ? a.qto.elements / a.count : 0;
        const primarySource: TakeoffRow["primarySource"] =
          qtoShare >= 0.5 ? "qto" : "geometry";
        const primary = primarySource === "qto" ? a.qto : a.geom;

        const variancePct =
          a.qto.volume > 0 && a.geom.volume > 0
            ? (Math.abs(a.qto.volume - a.geom.volume) / a.geom.volume) * 100
            : undefined;

        const precision =
          a.bboxOnly === 0
            ? "exact geometry"
            : a.bboxOnly === a.geom.elements
              ? "â‰ˆ bbox"
              : `â‰ˆ mixed (${a.geom.elements - a.bboxOnly} geo Â· ${a.bboxOnly} bbox)`;

        const detail =
          `${a.count} elements Â· model "${modelId}" Â· ${precision}` +
          (a.qto.elements ? ` Â· Qto ${a.qto.elements}/${a.count}` : "");

        out.push({
          key,
          source: "ifc",
          label: cat,
          detail,
          count: a.count,
          length: r3(primary.length),
          area: r3(primary.area),
          volume: r3(primary.volume),
          qto: a.qto.elements
            ? {
                length: r3(a.qto.length),
                area: r3(a.qto.area),
                volume: r3(a.qto.volume),
                elements: a.qto.elements,
              }
            : undefined,
          geom: a.geom.elements
            ? {
                length: r3(a.geom.length),
                area: r3(a.geom.area),
                volume: r3(a.geom.volume),
                elements: a.geom.elements,
              }
            : undefined,
          primarySource,
          variancePct,
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[cost] takeoff failed for model:", modelId, e);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Public
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

export async function computeTakeoff(): Promise<TakeoffRow[]> {
  const authored = authoredRows();
  const ifc = await ifcRows();
  rows = [...authored, ...ifc];
  return rows;
}

export function r3Compat(n: number) {
  return r3(n);
}
